# Project Documentation

## Recent Changes

- [2026-07-24]: **Quantitative-targets feature — adversarial PRE-MERGE review + the fixes it found**
  - *Details*: Before merging the whole targets + macro-goals feature to main, ran a read-only adversarial code review (6 reviewers across risk dimensions → each finding refuted-or-confirmed by 2 skeptics → merge-readiness synthesis; 37 agents, 15 raw findings, 12 survived). Verdict: **GO_WITH_FIXES** — 2 blocking defects, both in `desktop/lib/features/dashboard/data/dashboard_repository.dart`, both from the earlier desktop-domain slice (NOT the subagents), both the same class: a brand-new schema object accessed UNGATED on an EXISTING code path (so the dark flag doesn't protect it), which breaks account-mode desktop for ALL users if the `20260724` migration hasn't landed yet. All fixed + re-verified green (mobile 567, desktop 588).
  - *Tech Notes (fixes)*:
    - **BLOCKING 1 — `refresh()` read of the new `goal_progress` table sank the whole dashboard.** The `from('goal_progress').select()` sat inside the shared, rethrowing `Future.wait`; a pre-migration project returns "relation does not exist", rejecting the whole batch, so goals/logs/moods all discard → permanent "sync failed" empty dashboard. FIX: isolated it into `_fetchProgressRows()` (own try/catch, degrades to `[]` on any error, kicked off concurrently), mirroring how mobile already isolates the same read. A missing `goal_progress` now degrades only progress, never the dashboard.
    - **BLOCKING 2 — `updateHabit` force-wrote `goals.target` on EVERY edit, ungated.** `payload['target'] = habit.targetColumnValue` inserts an explicit `target: null` for a plain habit; against a pre-migration `goals` table PostgREST rejects the unknown column and the edit is LOST (not retryable). FIX: gated behind `if (DesktopTargetsConfig.enabled)`, matching the sibling macro force-write. Plain-habit edits stay column-free/migration-independent while dark.
    - **Coupled HIGH non-blocker (fixed for consistency)** — mobile `updateHabit` was the inverse: it NEVER force-wrote `target`, so once live, removing a target wouldn't clear it in Supabase (stale resurrects on sync). FIX: added the SAME flag-gated force-write to mobile's `goal_provider.updateHabit`. Both platforms now force-clear a removed target only when the flag is on — consistent AND pre-migration safe.
    - **Remaining survived findings are LOW / dark-gated / cosmetic → go-live checklist (TO_SIMO_DO), not merge blockers**: the resume-time reconcile sweep is ungated (a rollout-window UX artifact on a mixed-version fleet, no data loss); `deleteHabit` does snapshot+delete as two awaits rather than one transaction (narrow crash window, recoverable); a PRIVATE/CloudKit two-device create/delete race can leave a `linked_goal_id` dangling (FK enforcement is off during sync apply, so ON DELETE SET NULL doesn't fire on the pulling device); desktop account-mode delete-snapshot derives from the in-memory snapshot vs mobile's live SUM (multi-device stale-snapshot nuance); the reminder rotation-index picked a different (still valid) motivational line after a refactor (cosmetic). All independently confirmed real but non-blocking.
    - **Verification**: analyze clean on both apps (only pre-existing issues); affected test files + both FULL suites green after the fixes (mobile 567, desktop 588). No new tests were needed (the fixes are gating/robustness on Supabase paths not unit-tested with a live client; the logic is covered by the fake-repo tests).

- [2026-07-24]: **Quantitative habit targets — foundation: `evolve_targets` package + private schema v9 + Supabase `goal_progress`**
  - *Details*: First two slices of the "habits are not only checkboxes" feature. A habit may now carry a numeric target — *count* ("80 push-ups, in sets of 20"), *duration* ("20 minutes"), or *limit* ("at most 1 coffee") — instead of being a pure done/missed/pending checkbox. Rather than four habit TYPES with four code paths, a target is one object with four axes: **direction** (`atLeast`/`atMost`) × **period** (`day`/`week`/`month`) × **aggregation** (`sum`/`count`) × **fillSource** (`manual`/`healthKit`/`screenTime`), chosen through a curated preset catalog so the matrix never reaches the user. A future kind ("4 gym sessions per week", "≤3 beers per week", "500 km per year") is ONE registry entry plus its translations — no migration, no model change. The direction/unit/aggregation enums are **aliases of `evolve_verification`'s**, not copies, so a manually-counted target and an auto-verified threshold share one wire vocabulary and one verdict table; `targetFromVerificationRule` projects a rule into a target, which is what will let "10 000 steps" and "80 push-ups" render as the same progress ring with no duplicated renderer.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **New package `packages/evolve_targets/`** (pure Dart, depends only on `flutter` + `evolve_verification`; acyclic — verification knows nothing of targets). `HabitTarget` + versioned JSON codec (`{v,src,dir,per,agg,amount,unit,step,input,preset}`) that **preserves unknown keys** so a newer client's field survives an older client's edit (sync serialises whole rows, so that round-trip is ordinary); decode is *totally strict* — an unrecognised axis value rejects the whole blob and the habit reads as an ordinary checkbox, deliberately stricter than `VerificationAggregation.fromWire`, which defaults unknown input to `sum`. `TargetVerdict`/`evaluateTarget` mirror `VerificationService`'s decision table with exactly one source-dependent difference: **absent progress is zero for a manual target and silence for a measured one**, so a manual limit day with no entries resolves to `met` ("I consumed nothing") while a Screen Time day with no samples resolves to `unknown`, never a success. `TargetVerdict.logStatus` is the single place the progress→`goal_logs.status` mapping lives (the recon found that transition independently re-implemented in six places). `TargetPresetCatalog` ships 4 presets; `daysInPeriod`/`periodIsOver` already handle week/month so a quota is additive. **78 tests, analyzer clean.**
    - **`PrivateDbSchema` v8 → v9**: `goals.target TEXT` (nullable, unconstrained — a newer client's target must round-trip, not be rejected) and a new synced table **`goal_progress`** (`id, user_id, goal_id, date, amount, source`, `UNIQUE(goal_id,date)`). Registered in `syncedTables`, `_applyPriority` (2, child of goals) and `naturalKeys`. Migration is additive + idempotent (survives a version round-trip), and a v6 database migrates 6→7→8→9 in one open. **+14 schema tests; 220 `evolve_sync` tests green.**
    - **Why progress is NOT on `goal_logs`** (the load-bearing decision): `goal_logs.status` is CHECK-constrained to `('done','missed','skipped')` on BOTH backends, so a 40-of-80 day has no honest status — writing `'missed'` would enter four rate denominators that count only *logged* rows, making an engaged-but-unfinished user score worse than one who ignores the habit, and widening the CHECK is worse still (the sync engine quarantines unrecognised statuses on every un-upgraded device). `goal_logs.value` is not free either: three guards null it, one of them *intentionally* on every manual toggle (`private_local_database.dart:526`, `ConflictAlgorithm.replace`), and mobile and desktop already disagree about it. A separate table leaves `goal_logs` as purely the VERDICT record, so **all 11 Supabase analytics objects, both Dart mirrors, every heatmap and every older client keep working untouched**, and an unfinished day renders its ring while staying out of every denominator.
    - **Deterministic `goal_progress.id` = `'<goal_id>:<date>'`** (`PrivateDbSchema.goalProgressId`, the `user_settings` pattern). Not cosmetic: with random ids, two devices logging the same habit-day mint rival rows for one natural key and the pull resolves that by DELETING the loser and tombstoning it. A shared id downgrades that to ordinary row-level LWW. `source` on the row also turns the "Apple health measurements never reach Supabase" rule into a fact about the row instead of today's guess through the goal (`?? true`, which defaults an unresolvable goal to "health").
    - **Supabase**: new `migrations/20260724_add_goal_targets_and_progress.sql` (goals.target + goal_progress + 4 RLS policies + updated_at trigger, all `IF NOT EXISTS`). **No analytics RPC changed** — a target habit still materialises an ordinary `done`/`missed` row when its day resolves. `schema.sql` updated for parity, including the `verify_effective_from` / `verify_conditions` columns this branch had added to the private schema but never mirrored there.
    - **Wiring**: `evolve_targets` added as a path dependency to both apps. Mobile **489 tests green**, desktop **544 green**, both analyzers clean (desktop's 4 issues are the pre-existing ones already logged).
  - *Current Status*: BOTH features are now COMPLETE end-to-end on both platforms and INDEPENDENTLY VERIFIED green — the quantitative-HABIT-targets feature (model, storage, sweep, both domain layers, both UIs, backup/import, target-aware notifications) dark behind `TargetsConfig`/`DesktopTargetsConfig`, and the cumulative numeric MACRO-goals feature (#6: storage, domain, both progress read paths, both UIs, delete-snapshot) dark behind `MacroTargetsConfig`/`DesktopMacroTargetsConfig`. All four flags are `false`; nothing is user-visible in shipping builds until they are flipped after on-device QA. Independently-verified suites: evolve_sync **229**, evolve_targets **103**, mobile **567**, desktop **588** — all green. Remaining is manual/owner: apply the 4 queued Supabase migrations, flip the flags after on-device QA, Arabic native review, and a separate pass on two PRE-EXISTING parallel-load test flakes (`desktop/test/evolve_controls_test.dart` and `subscription_entitlement_scope_test.dart` — both pass in isolation/on retry, neither a feature regression).

- [2026-07-24]: **Cumulative numeric macro goals (feature #6) — UI + closing the two domain gaps (dark)**
  - *Details*: Completes feature #6 — the create/edit form (numeric target amount + unit + optional link-a-habit picker), the progress bar, and the two gaps the foundation left (cloud-mode delete-snapshot, force-write-on-update). Dark behind `MacroTargetsConfig.enabled` / `DesktopMacroTargetsConfig.enabled`; with the flags off every macro-goal surface is byte-for-byte today's boolean behaviour.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **Derived-progress read path (the crux)**: a numeric macro goal's displayed progress is a STORED column when manual, but for a LINKED goal it is the SUM of the linked habit's `goal_progress.amount` over the goal's period — a per-mode DB read, not a column. Mobile: `PrivateDataStore.linkedHabitProgressSum` (private, over SQLCipher) + `supabase_macro_goal_progress.dart` (account, sums Supabase `goal_progress`) + pure `macro_goal_progress_resolver.dart` + `macroGoalProgressProvider` (batches all numeric goals, watches `habitProgressProvider` so a linked bar updates live). Desktop: `features/dashboard/domain/macro_goal_progress.dart` derives the total as a pure in-memory reduction over the `habitProgress` map the snapshot already loads (no per-goal query). Both feed `evaluateMacroGoalProgress`.
    - **UI**: mobile `ui/widgets/macro_goals/macro_target_field.dart` (`MacroTargetField`) wired into `add_goal_bar.dart` (create) + `goal_item_widget.dart` (edit + `_buildProgressBar`); `macro_goals_provider.updateGoalTarget`. Desktop `create_goal_dialog.dart` + `goals_page.dart` `_GoalEditorDialog`/`_MacroGoalProgressBar` + `dashboard_controller` addGoal/updateGoal target params.
    - **Cloud delete-snapshot** (the deferred gap, now closed): mobile `goal_provider.deleteHabit` and desktop `SupabaseDashboardRepository.deleteHabit` freeze a linked goal's derived total into `progress_amount` before the FK SET NULL un-links it — in-memory sum, zero extra round-trips, and a no-op while nothing is linked.
    - **Force-write-on-update — DELIBERATELY GATED (verified)**: unlike the ungated habit-`target` fix, the macro-goal UPDATE force-write of the numeric columns is inside `if (…MacroTargetsConfig.enabled)` on BOTH apps. Reason: those columns exist only after the pending `20260724_add_macro_goal_targets` migration, so an ungated force-write would send them on every plain-goal rename and break those UPDATEs in production until the migration lands. Gated, it's inert while dark and clears correctly once live. **⇒ the Supabase migration MUST be applied before the flag is flipped** (logged in TO_SIMO_DO). The independent verification agent confirmed the gating by reading the actual lines.
    - **i18n**: new `macroTargets.*` namespace (sectionTitle/none/amountLabel/linkLabel/manual/unitCount/reached) in all 5 locales both apps; Arabic machine MSA, flagged for review.
    - **Verification**: +16 mobile, +12 desktop tests. Mobile **567**, desktop **588** — INDEPENDENTLY re-verified by a dedicated verification-only agent (read-only), which also confirmed all suites green, feature dark, migrations present, schema v10, and characterised the two pre-existing parallel-load test flakes (see Current Status) as non-regressions.

- [2026-07-24]: **Cumulative numeric macro goals (feature #6) — storage/domain/import foundation (dark)**
  - *Details*: A macro goal (`long_term_goals`) can now carry an optional numeric target ("run 500 km this year") whose progress is fed AUTOMATICALLY by a linked habit's daily `goal_progress`, or entered manually. This is the foundation slice — storage, models, the progress-evaluation helper, backup/import, and private-mode delete-snapshot — all ungated at the domain layer and tested. The UI is deferred behind a new dark flag.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **Storage (private v9→v10 + Supabase)**: `long_term_goals` gains 4 nullable columns — `target_amount` (numeric; null ⇒ ordinary boolean macro goal, unchanged), `target_unit` (`TargetUnit` wire name, unconstrained), `progress_amount` (STORED value for a manual goal + the snapshot slot on unlink), `linked_goal_id` (FK → `goals(id)` **ON DELETE SET NULL** — verified the SET NULL fires the parent sync trigger so the un-link auto-syncs). `PrivateDbSchema` v10 (`_upgradeToV10`, additive+idempotent, guards a missing table like `_upgradeToV5`); `migrations/20260724_add_macro_goal_targets.sql`; `schema.sql`. +9 evolve_sync schema tests.
    - **Progress helper** (`packages/evolve_targets/lib/src/macro_goal_progress.dart`, +12 tests): pure `evaluateMacroGoalProgress` — `atLeast`-only; effective progress = the linked habit's summed `goal_progress` over the goal's PERIOD when linked, else the stored `progress_amount`; fraction clamped for a bar, `rawFraction` keeps overachievement; period→range via a new `macroGoalPeriodRange` (annual/quarterly/monthly = calendar range; weekly reuses `logicalWeekRange`; lifetime = all history).
    - **Models + import**: `MacroGoal` (mobile) + `DashboardGoal` (desktop) gain the fields + round-trip + copyWith; private-DB read/write updated. The 4 columns added to EVERY macro-goal allowlist across both apps' import pipelines (normalizers, validate, `_macroRow`, private merge, cloud plan, exports).
    - **Delete-snapshot** (`macro_goal_snapshot.dart`, both apps): before deleting a habit in PRIVATE mode, any macro goal linked to it has its derived progress summed and written into `progress_amount` so the accumulated value survives as a now-manual number. **Cloud/account mode is DEFERRED** — there, deleting a linked habit relies on Postgres ON DELETE SET NULL (un-links) but does NOT snapshot, so an account-mode user would lose the accumulated derived value; needs a client fetch-sum-before-delete (or a PG trigger). Low urgency (feature ships dark).
    - **DEFERRED (main remaining work)**: (1) the UI — no create/edit numeric-target field, link-a-habit picker, or progress bar on either app; the dark flags `MacroTargetsConfig`/`DesktopMacroTargetsConfig` are the gate. This is the bulk of the remaining feature and will add the i18n keys (5 locales). (2) cloud-mode delete-snapshot (above). (3) `get_macro_goals_stats` RPC + Dart `computeMacroGoalsStats` twins were NOT changed — the agent's reasoning (they score by `status`, and a numeric goal is completed via the same `status`, so stats stay correct; reflecting numeric *progress* in stats is an enhancement, not a fix) is sound. (4) `toJson`/`toRemoteJson` emit the numeric keys only when non-null (so a plain-goal insert works before the migration lands) — so a Supabase UPDATE can't actively CLEAR a target/link yet; apply the habit-`target` force-write-on-update pattern when the UI ships.
    - **Verification**: evolve_sync **229**, evolve_targets **103**, mobile **551**, desktop **576** — all green (orchestrator re-ran all four independently). NOTE: `desktop/test/evolve_controls_test.dart` (pre-existing control-widget tests, untouched by this work) shows INTERMITTENT flakiness (2–3 tap/render tests fail on some full-suite runs, pass on retry and in isolation) — borderline `pumpAndSettle` timing surfacing under the larger suite's parallel load, not a feature regression. Worth a separate test-hardening pass.

- [2026-07-24]: **Quantitative habit targets — target-aware notifications + a live provenance-bug fix**
  - *Details*: Two fixes in the reminder/notification path. (1) A LIMIT habit ("≤1 coffee") no longer gets achievement-framed "do it!" reminder pushes on the days it is succeeding by consuming nothing — it now gets restraint-framed copy ("Staying within your limit today?"). Gated behind the targets dark flag, so mainline is unchanged. (2) A LIVE, shipping bug (not behind any flag): a notification "Done"/"Skip" on an auto-VERIFIED habit was silently reverted by the next foreground reconcile, because the notification write path never recorded the D9 manual-freeze that `cycleStatus` does.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **Reminder copy**: `notifications.dart` (mobile) + `desktop_notification_service.dart` (desktop) gained a pure, testable `reminderBody(...)` that picks restraint copy when `habit.target?.isLimit == true`, gated by a `featureEnabled` param that DEFAULTS to the compile-time `TargetsConfig.enabled` / `DesktopTargetsConfig.enabled` const — so the limit branch tree-shakes in production and tests exercise it with `featureEnabled: true`. `isLimit` threaded through the 4 mobile `scheduleHabitReminder` call sites (`goal_provider.dart`, `settings_provider.dart`) + the desktop scheduler. Count/duration targets keep the existing motivational copy (only the limit inversion was the bug); snooze re-fire copy unchanged (payload carries no target info).
    - **Provenance fix (mobile only)**: `notifications.dart`'s `_writeHabitLogFromNotification` now calls `_freezeManualForToday(habitId)` (both Done→done and Skip→missed), so the foreground reconcile leaves the notification-set day alone. Verified safe: the reconcile SKIPS manual days entirely and only reads manual-freeze for currently-verifiable goals' ids, so a freeze written for a plain habit is harmless dead data — meaning the notification path needn't load the goal to check `isVerified`. Gated on `VerificationConfig.enabled` (true — HealthKit is live, so this is a real shipping bug) and fully try-caught (a store failure can't break the log write). Refactor: extracted a shared `SqfliteVerificationStateStore.open()` used by both `verification_providers.dart` and the notification path. Desktop has no verification reconcile, so the bug is inherently mobile-only — nothing to fix there.
    - **i18n**: new limit-reminder strings in all 5 locales — mobile `notifications.limitReminderMessage{1,2,3}`, desktop `notif.limitReminderBody`. Arabic is machine MSA, flagged for native review.
    - **Verification**: +8 mobile tests (`reminder_copy_test.dart`, `notification_manual_freeze_test.dart`), +4 desktop (`reminder_copy_test.dart`). Mobile **534 green**, desktop **568 green**; analyzers clean bar pre-existing. Orchestrator independently re-ran analyze + the new tests on both apps to confirm.

- [2026-07-24]: **Quantitative habit targets — backup/export/import (both apps)**
  - *Details*: A targeted habit must round-trip through backup/restore, or it silently reverts to a plain checkbox (the recon flagged this as a silent, uncounted data loss). Added `goals.target` to every column-allowlist across the export/import pipelines, and made the new `goal_progress` table round-trip too (so a restore keeps the rings, not just the done/missed verdicts that live in `goal_logs`).
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **Mobile** (`import_merge.dart`, `backup_import_service.dart`, `private_local_database.dart`): `target` added to both normalizers, `validateCanonical`, `_goalRow`, the cloud plan's goal row, and `exportData`. New `kProgressKey`; `goal_progress` normalized (from `habitProgress`/`goal_progress`), validated (positive-amount required), merged by natural key `(goal_id,date)` LWW with a deterministic `goalId:date` id, wiped on replace, and carried through the cloud plan + upserted in `backup_import_service` (fetch `existingProgress`, `_bulkUpsert`, delete-complement). No new stats counter — progress is a sub-detail of a habit-day, not a reported entity — so no stats/UI/i18n ripple.
    - **Desktop** (`import_merge.dart`, `desktop_backup_import_service.dart`, `desktop_private_db.dart`): the same shape across desktop's THREE allowlists — `_normalizeShape`, `validateCanonical`, AND `_processData` (a third column-rebuild the mobile side doesn't have). Found via a failing round-trip test: `_processData` rebuilds goal rows column-by-column and was dropping `target` even though normalize/validate carried it — exactly the "silent strip" pattern the recon warned about. `exportData`, `applyImport` (target via `_goalRow`, progress merge + wipe), and the cloud plan/service all updated.
    - **Health-privacy**: `goal_progress` is manual-only (source `'manual'`, never a device measurement), so — unlike `goal_logs.value` — it needs no `stripHealthMeasurements` and is safe to upload to a cloud account. (The pre-existing desktop-cloud-import gap where `goal_logs.value` is uploaded without stripping is untouched — out of scope for targets, and targets add nothing to it.)
    - **Verification**: +6 mobile (`import_merge_test.dart`) and +3 desktop (`backup_roundtrip_test.dart`) round-trip tests — target persists, `goal_progress` deterministic id, orphan/positive-amount/replace-wipe. Mobile **526 green**, desktop **564 green**; analyzers clean bar pre-existing.

  - *Details*: The desktop twin of the mobile UI, bringing macOS to full parity — a target picker in the habit editor and a progress ring you click to log against, on both the Habits Protocol list and the dashboard "today" list. Ships dark behind `desktop/lib/core/targets_config.dart` (`enabled = false`), matching mobile so the two apps flip together.
  - *Tech Notes*:

Both habit-row check squares — `habits_page.dart` `_HabitRow` (Protocol) and `dashboard_page.dart` `_HabitRow` (today) — render the ring for a MANUAL target and open the entry dialog on tap (measured/projected rules keep the checkbox, same reasoning as mobile).

I built FULL PARITY instead — desktop increments too — because the increment path already existed (`setHabitProgressForDay`, used by the sweep), the owner's standing preference is full coherence, and the race is the same class as any two-device edit the app already tolerates via the natural-key merge. A completed target writes 'done', so it shows done everywhere. Polish, not correctness.

  - *Details*: The first user-facing slice — a habit can be given a target in the create/edit sheet, and a target habit's day-details card shows a progress ring you tap to log against. Ships **dark** behind `mobile/lib/core/targets_config.dart` (`enabled = false`), the same dark-launch pattern as verification/compound: the DOMAIN layer is never gated (a `goals.target` from a device where the flag is live must round-trip), only the UI is.

This advances task #8 for mobile; the Arabic strings are machine-quality and flagged for native review in `TO_SIMO_DO.md`. slang regenerated.

(2) The five READ-ONLY surfaces (month calendar, weekly radar, yearly painter, stats heatmap, AI-coach prompt) still paint an in-progress day as an ordinary empty/pending day — they are not WRONG (a partial day genuinely is pending, and a completed target writes 'done' so it shows done everywhere), they just don't show the ring/fraction. Polish, not correctness.

- [2026-07-24]: **Quantitative habit targets — end-of-day resolution sweep (both platforms)**
  - *Details*: The piece that makes LIMIT habits work unattended. A limit day ("≤1 coffee") is a success only knowable once the day is over — and the live increment path can only mark today's verdict while the day is still open (pending). If the user never reopens the app before midnight, that quiet day would never get its `done` verdict, and the habit would look perpetually unlogged/failed. The sweep materialises those verdicts on app foreground.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **Shared pure sweep** (`packages/evolve_targets/src/target_reconcile.dart`): `reconcileManualTargetDays(...)` walks the closed, scheduled days in a backfill window and returns the days whose stored verdict doesn't match their stored progress. Rules: today is never swept (live-derived); off-schedule days never get a verdict (hide-off-day invariant); a **limit** day with no progress is a quiet SUCCESS → `done`; an **atLeast** day with no progress is SKIPPED so an untouched count habit stays absent like a checkbox (its streak already breaks on the absent row) rather than being sprayed with `missed`; only changed days are emitted (idempotent). `kManualTargetBackfillDays = 45` bounds both coverage and the "opened after weeks away → free streak" effect. +13 package tests.
    - **Appliers reuse the live path** (one write path, not a second to keep in step): mobile `HabitProgressNotifier.reconcileManualTargets({now})` applies each change through `setProgress`; desktop `DashboardController.reconcileManualTargets({now})` through `setHabitProgressForDay`. Both re-derive and persist exactly as a live edit would, streaks included, applied sequentially so streaks build in date order.
    - **Triggers**: mobile from the `AppLifecycleState.resumed` hook in `main.dart` (independent of the verification flag and of data mode — a manual target is plain local data that works in both); desktop from the tail of `DashboardController.refresh()` (covers cold start + any refresh).
    - **DESKTOP RUNS IT TOO — deliberate.** The recon's "macOS has no reconcile" is about *verification* (a device measurement iOS owns); a manual target is local data already synced to the Mac, so a Mac-primary user's limit habits must resolve there or they'd look perpetually unlogged. Cross-device double-writes of the same quiet `done` converge under the existing natural-key merge (idempotent, same value). Owner can veto the desktop half — it is one self-contained driver + the `refresh()` tail.
    - **Robustness fix found in passing**: `refresh()`'s fire-and-forget reconcile tail could touch `state` after the controller was disposed (data-mode switch / sign-out / test teardown) and throw. Added a `_disposed` flag (set in `ref.onDispose`) guarding the loop and the tail.
    - **Verification**: +4 mobile, +3 desktop reconcile tests (quiet-days→done, atLeast-untouched-skipped, partial-pending→missed, idempotent). Suites: evolve_targets **91**, mobile **510**, desktop **555**, all green; analyzers clean bar the pre-existing issues.

- [2026-07-24]: **Quantitative habit targets — desktop (macOS) domain wiring**
  - *Details*: The desktop counterpart of the mobile domain slice, adapted to desktop's repository pattern. Same architecture: progress is a SEPARATE parallel map on the snapshot (`DashboardSnapshot.habitProgress`) mirroring `goal_progress`, so the many `== 'done'` readers of `habitLogs` are untouched. Nothing user-visible yet.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **Model** (`dashboard_models.dart`): `DashboardHabit` gains `target` + `rawTargetBlob` (+ `hasTarget`/`displayTarget`/`targetColumnValue`, copyWith `target`/`clearTarget`, `toRemoteJson`/`fromRemoteJson`), mirroring mobile's `Goal` incl. the forward-compat blob preservation. `DashboardSnapshot` gains `habitProgress: Map<String,Map<String,double>>` + `habitProgressFor(...)` + copyWith.
    - **Repositories**: new `setHabitProgress({habitId, date, amount, derivedStatus, streak})` on the base (no-op), proxy (delegates), unavailable (`_requireSession`), Supabase and private. Both real backends persist the `goal_progress` number AND the derived `goal_logs` verdict in one call — deterministic id `'<goalId>:<date>'`, update-or-insert to dodge the REPLACE tombstone, `goal_logs.value` never written. Supabase `refresh()` now fetches `goal_progress` (5th query), `_fromRemote`/`_toCache`/`_fromCache` carry the progress map (with a pre-targets-cache fallback to empty), `updateHabit` force-writes `target` (UPDATE leaves omitted columns untouched — same fix as `frequency_days`). Private `refresh`/`_buildSnapshot`/`_habitFromRow` read progress + target; `resetData` wipes `goal_progress`.
    - **Controller** (`dashboard_controller.dart`): `setHabitProgressForDay(id, date, amount, {now})` — the desktop counterpart of mobile's `HabitProgressNotifier.setProgress`. Optimistic + local-first like `toggleHabitForDay`: updates the progress map, derives the verdict via `evaluateTarget`/`TargetVerdict.logStatus`, moves the logs map + weekly grid + streak, then syncs. No-op for a measured target (its ring is filled by the verification pipeline). NOTE: in cloud mode this writes the controller's `computeStreak` result directly — so a target habit's streak is *correct*, unlike the checkbox path which still routes through the naive `_nextStreak` (pre-existing debt, untouched here).
    - **Verification**: +8 desktop tests (`dashboard_target_progress_test.dart` — open/closed partials, limit resolution, pending-today, zero-return, measured no-op). Desktop suite **552 green**; `flutter analyze lib/` clean bar the 3 pre-existing issues.

- [2026-07-24]: **Quantitative habit targets — mobile domain wiring**
  - *Details*: Wired the target model + `goal_progress` storage into the mobile app's domain layer, WITHOUT widening the 96 `== 'done'` verdict call-sites. The key architectural choice: progress is a SEPARATE parallel provider (`habitProgressProvider`) mirroring the separate `goal_progress` table, so the status map (`HabitLogsMap`, the verdict currency) is untouched. Everything that reads "is this day done" keeps reading the verdict string; only the surfaces that will draw a ring read the new progress map. Nothing is user-visible yet — this is the plumbing the UI slice will sit on.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **`Goal` model** (`mobile/lib/models/goal.dart`): new `HabitTarget? target` + `String? rawTargetBlob`. The blob is the forward-compat guard — a target written by a NEWER client (an axis value this build can't decode, so `target` is null) is written back **verbatim** on an unrelated edit instead of being nulled, closing a gap the v8 `verify_conditions` path still has. `targetColumnValue` getter (live target encoded → unreadable blob verbatim → null) is shared by the cloud (`toJson`) and private (`_goalToRow`) write paths so they can't disagree. `copyWith`: setting a target supersedes a preserved blob; `clearTarget` wipes both; a title-only copy preserves both. `displayTarget` getter projects a single verification rule when there's no manual target (unifies verified thresholds with manual counts).
    - **Progress provider** (`goal_provider.dart`): `HabitProgressMap = Map<String,Map<String,double>>`; `HabitProgressNotifier` mirrors `HabitLogsNotifier` (private load + Supabase paginated sync + keychain cache `goal_progress_cache`, prewarmed in `main.dart` via `initialProgressProvider`). `setProgress(...)` persists the number to the active backend AND derives the verdict via the new `HabitLogsNotifier.setDerivedStatus(...)`, which sets/clears the `goal_logs` row (never writing `goal_logs.value` — a manual number is not a health measurement) and recomputes the streak. Deterministic Supabase id `'<goalId>:<dateKey>'` matches the private store's.
    - **Private store** (`private_local_database.dart` + `PrivateDataStore` interface + fake): `loadHabitProgress` / `setHabitProgress` (deterministic id, `ConflictAlgorithm.replace`) / `deleteHabitProgress`; `_goalFromRow`/`_goalToRow` carry `target`; `deleteAllPrivateData` wipes `goal_progress` explicitly beside `goal_logs`.
    - **Verdict semantics preserved from the shared package**: a partial OPEN day writes no verdict row (stays out of every denominator); a partial CLOSED day is an honest 'missed'; a manual limit day resolves only at period end (never green from breakfast); manual absence = 0, measured absence = unknown.
    - **Verification**: +17 mobile tests (`goal_target_model_test.dart` incl. the forward-compat preservation, `habit_progress_notifier_test.dart` incl. limit/count/zero/open-vs-closed). Mobile suite **506 green**; `flutter analyze lib/` clean bar the 4 pre-existing `macro_goals_screen.dart` issues.

- [2026-07-23]: **AI coach engine picker redesign (desktop/macOS) — engine cards + per-product model memory**
  - *Details*: Reworked how the AI coach LLM is chosen so Ollama and LM Studio are first-class rather than buried behind a preset dropdown. The old header chip opened a bespoke quick-menu that could only show the ONE local server `localBaseUrl` pointed at, so switching Ollama↔LM Studio meant a trip through the settings popup (the exact asymmetry vs. always-visible BYOK that the user flagged). Now the chat-header chip is a professional **state-selector** (engine icon + active model + a live status dot: green reachable / amber needs-setup / grey off) that opens the reorganized **"AI coach engine"** popup directly — no dropdown. The popup's top is a row of **engine cards**, mode-scoped exactly as before (account mode = Evolve AI only; Private mode = OpenRouter + Ollama + LM Studio), each probed for live status; picking one activates it live and shows its detail below. "Custom / other OpenAI-compatible server" moved behind a **"Use a custom server…"** link — the base-URL + manual-model fields are hidden for the named products (their card *is* the URL). Selecting an off product shows the existing **"Start {app}"** launch-poll flow (no auto-launch on mere selection — the honest model for LM Studio's opt-in server). Both in-chat banners (offline-recovery + detected-local nudge) kept. The App Store Guideline 3.1.1 data-mode/backend split is preserved untouched.
  - *Tech Notes*: Domain — `CoachConfig.localModel` (a single field) replaced by `localModels: Map<baseUrl→modelId>` with a derived `localModel` getter keyed by the active `localBaseUrl`, so each server remembers its own last model and a one-tap switch never strands a stale id the other product would answer with "model not found". New prefs key `coach_local_models` (JSON); a one-time migration seeds it from the retired `coach_local_model` string at the then-current URL, then clears that key. No new RPCs, edge functions, or dependencies. Auto-picks a lone discovered model on switch (post-frame, guarded) so a switch lands ready to chat. Files: `desktop/lib/features/ai_coach/domain/coach_config.dart`; `.../application/coach_controllers.dart` (map persistence + `_readLocalModels` migration); `.../presentation/coach_model_chip.dart` (selector + status dot → opens dialog; static `activeLabel` kept, so `settings_page.dart` is untouched and reflects the new dialog for free); `.../presentation/coach_settings_dialog.dart` (rewritten: `_StandardEngine`, `_PrivateEngines`, `_EngineCard`, `_CustomServerLink`, `_LocalDetail`). i18n: 6 new `coachSettings` keys (`localGroupLabel`, `useCustomServer`, `cardLive`, `cardOff`, `engineOpenRouter`, `engineOpenRouterHint`) across en/it/de/es/ar + `dart run slang`. Tests: +5 domain (per-URL memory), +3 controller (persistence + legacy migration), widget test rewritten to the card dialog (account + private modes). Full suite **543 green** (with dummy `EVOLVE_SUPABASE_*` dart-defines); `flutter analyze` clean (4 pre-existing issues in unrelated files). On-device visual QA pending — no Xcode / no Ollama|LM Studio installed on this Mac (see `TO_SIMO_DO.md`).

- [2026-07-23]: **iCloud sync — diagnosed "Mac nearly empty" as a CloudKit Dev/Prod environment split; pinned the macOS environment**
  - *Details*: On TestFlight, the Mac (private mode) showed almost no data while the iPhone had everything. Diagnosed (read-only, two exploration agents + the existing `ICLOUD_SYNC_STATE.md`/memory) as a **CloudKit Development-vs-Production datastore split**, NOT a key-split: the iPhone's bulk (3,489 macro goals / 2,591 goal_logs / 20 habits) was synced to the **Development** environment during earlier Xcode dev-build testing; TestFlight builds use **Production**. All those rows are `dirty=0` locally and the sync engine only pushes `dirty=1` with no re-backfill (`sync_engine.dart` `_push` → `dirtyEntries()` = `WHERE dirty=1`; `markAllDirty()` runs only once at enable), so the iPhone never re-uploaded the bulk to Production — the Mac only ever pulled the post-TestFlight trickle. The Mac decrypting *some* iPhone records confirms the shared E2E key is intact (rules out key-split). Dev→Prod is a developer-only artifact (real App Store users never touch Development CloudKit). Recovery is `resetSyncFromThisDevice()` **on the iPhone** (re-dirties all rows and re-uploads); it must never be run on the Mac (synchronizable key/zone delete would destroy the iPhone's copy). Recovery steps + the signing checklist are in `TO_SIMO_DO.md`.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **Root cause of the environment ambiguity**: neither app pins the CloudKit environment — there is no `com.apple.developer.icloud-container-environment` entitlement anywhere, so Dev-vs-Prod is chosen implicitly by the signing identity. The macOS Release config signs with "Apple Development" (`desktop/macos/Runner.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj:788`), which for a non-App-Store export would bind to Development.
    - **Fix implemented (Lever 1)**: pinned the environment explicitly on macOS — added `com.apple.developer.icloud-container-environment` = `Production` to `desktop/macos/Runner/Release.entitlements` and = `Development` to `DebugProfile.entitlements`. Now deterministic per build config: dev/local builds stay isolated in Development, release builds use Production. `plutil -lint` OK on both; ignored for App Store distribution (Production forced there) so it is safe belt-and-suspenders + dev isolation. iOS intentionally left unpinned (single shared `Runner.entitlements`; a naive pin would force Debug iOS onto Production, and iOS already selects the environment correctly by signing).
    - **No app/Dart code changed**; no dependencies. macOS signing (Lever 2) and the on-device recovery are owner tasks (no Xcode on this machine) — logged in `TO_SIMO_DO.md`.
    - **Key file-name gotcha (unchanged, for future readers)**: `goals` = habits, `long_term_goals` = macro goals.

- [2026-07-22]: **AI Coach Pro-gating made coherent across mobile & desktop + real desktop paywall**
  - *Details*: Enforced one policy on both apps: the coach's data modes now own disjoint backend sets. **Account mode** (signed in) = the Pro-funded dev-key proxy ONLY, gated by Pro — a signed-in user can no longer bring their own key, and a non-pro one meets the paywall. **Private mode** (no account) = free BYOK + Local (Ollama/LM Studio) only, unchanged. Mode picks the backend set; Pro only decides paywall-vs-access within account mode. Also replaced the desktop "View plans" action, which previously dead-ended by deep-linking into Settings (landing on Profile, and filtered out entirely in Private mode), with a real modal paywall mirroring mobile's `SubscriptionScreen`. Migration is non-destructive: existing signed-in BYOK/Local users keep their stored key/config (valid again in Private mode) and simply meet the paywall in account mode. The server-side proxy already enforced `profiles.is_pro`, so this is client-side gating UX + the desktop paywall, not a security change.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **Mobile**: `resolveCoachMode` (`lib/core/coach_endpoint.dart`) now returns BYOK only in Private mode and Standard only for a Pro account session (no BYOK fallback in account mode). Coach tile gate (`lib/ui/widgets/protocollo_panel.dart`) simplified to `!isPrivate && !isPro` (dropped the `hasKey` escape; removed the now-unused `openrouter_service` import). Settings (`lib/ui/screens/app_settings_screen.dart`) hides the OpenRouter key row in account mode and shows a note pointing to Private mode. New string `ai.apiKey.accountModeNote` (all 5 locales). Updated `test/coach_endpoint_test.dart` (2 cases flipped to the new policy).
    - **Desktop**: `effectiveCoachBackend` (`lib/features/ai_coach/domain/coach_config.dart`) now forces account mode → Standard always (Cloud/Local preserved but never serve while signed in); Private mode unchanged. New `coachNeedsPaywallProvider` (`lib/features/ai_coach/application/coach_controllers.dart`) — the shell gate (`desktop_shell.dart`) now `ref.watch`es it (was a non-reactive `ref.read` compound), fixing mid-session staleness for both the sidebar tap and ⌘5. Coach settings picker (`coach_settings_dialog.dart`) is mode-scoped: account = Standard only + a note; Private = Cloud/Local. New `showPaywallDialog` (`settings_page.dart`) reuses the existing `_SubscriptionSettings` (single source of truth for pricing/purchase/restore/compliance) with a new `showFeaturePitch:false` flag; `pro_features_modal.dart` CTA now opens it. New string `coachSettings.accountModeNote` and reworded `coachSettings.standardNeedsProNote` (all 5 locales). Updated `test/coach_config_test.dart` + `test/coach_config_controller_test.dart` for the account-mode-is-Standard-only rule.
    - **Verification**: `flutter analyze` clean on both apps (0 new issues); `flutter test` green (desktop 76, mobile 25). slang regenerated in both apps. No new dependencies. Manual items (macOS IAP availability + on-device QA) logged in `TO_SIMO_DO.md`.

Added two layers of defense: a paywall gate at the tile entry point and a typed exception handler for `not_subscribed` errors in the chat screen.

- [2026-07-19]: **Desktop Lateral Scroll Navigation (Macro Goals)**
  - *Details*: Implemented two-finger lateral scroll navigation on the desktop Macro Goals page to allow users to switch periods (weeks/months/years) via trackpad swipe, mirroring the mobile experience.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - Added a `Listener` around `EvolvePeriodSwitcher` in `goals_page.dart` to capture `PointerScrollEvent` and `PointerPanZoomUpdateEvent`.
    - Tracked `_horizontalScrollDelta` with a `40.0` pixel threshold to trigger period changes via `_movePeriod()`.
    - Added a 300ms cooldown using `_lastSwipeTime` to prevent skipping multiple months with a single fast swipe, explicitly clearing the inertia accumulator to prevent bleed-through.
    - Swiping right (pulling content right) reveals the left (past) -> `_movePeriod(-1)`. Swiping left reveals the right (future) -> `_movePeriod(1)`.
    - Ignored primarily vertical scrolling (`dy.abs() > dx.abs()`) to avoid disrupting normal list interaction.
    - Updated `desktop_shell.dart`'s `_onTrackpadPanUpdate` to ignore swipe navigation if the current section is `DesktopSection.goals` or `DesktopSection.statistics`, allowing the calendar and tabs to fully consume the horizontal swipe gesture without inadvertently navigating back in the app history.
    - Extended the lateral swipe feature to the `GoalsStatsView` widget, allowing users to scroll between "All Time" and available specific years (locked behind Pro feature checks where applicable).
    - Added the lateral swipe feature to `StatisticsPage` to allow seamless tab switching across both global (`_GlobalTab`) and habit-specific (`_HabitTab`) scopes.
    - Implemented a full-window dual-zone lateral swipe logic in `GoalsPage`: Swiping anywhere outside the goals list switches between `GoalType`s (e.g., Weekly -> Monthly), while swiping over the goals list itself switches the calendar period (e.g., Next/Previous Week).
    - Extended the lateral swipe feature to the `HabitsPage`, enabling users to quickly toggle between the `Protocol` and `Calendar` views (`_HabitSurface`) by swiping anywhere on the page.

- [2026-07-19]: **Screen Time Auto-Verification Logic Update (At Least vs At Most)**
  - *Details*: Fixed the logic in `VerificationService.evaluateScreenTimeDay` to accurately map iOS `DeviceActivityMonitor` signals (`reachedThreshold` and `stayedUnder`) based on the habit's type: Limits (At most) vs Goals (At least).
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - Limits: `reachedThreshold` -> `fail()`, `stayedUnder` -> `pass()`.
    - Goals: `reachedThreshold` -> `pass()`, `stayedUnder` -> `fail()`.
    - Updated `blockedFlip` logic to ensure that once a permanent state (e.g. `fail` for Limits, or `pass` for Goals) is reached, it is not overturned by duplicate or late signals.
    - Updated `day_verdict_test.dart` to cover both `VerificationComparator.atLeast` and `VerificationComparator.atMost` scenarios.
    - Updated `VerificationRuleField` to include an `EvolveSegmentedControl` for Screen Time habits, allowing users to toggle between Goal (At least) and Limit (At most).
    - Added translations for `comparatorAtLeast` and `comparatorAtMost` across all 5 languages.
    - Fixed a UI stale cache issue where `screenTimeAuthStatusProvider` wouldn't clear the "needs access" warning after returning from Settings. Added an `AppLifecycleListener` to the `FutureProvider` so it auto-invalidates and reads fresh OS authorization status on `onResume`.
    - Omitted background sync via `BGTaskScheduler` per user request to only track from iOS and visualize on macOS; opening the iOS app triggers `runVerificationReconcile` and syncs the data to Supabase.

    - Added the corresponding `"NSHealthUpdateUsageDescription"` and `"NSLocationWhenInUseUsageDescription"` translations into `en`, `ar`, `es`, `de`, and `it` `InfoPlist.strings` files inside `mobile/ios/Runner/`.
    - Verified all `.strings` syntax and ran `plutil -lint` successfully on `Info.plist`.

- [2026-07-18]: **Screen Time Authorization UX Fix**
  - *Details*: Fixed a bug where trying to authorize a Screen Time habit when the system had denied/revoked the permission would repeatedly throw an unhandled `PlatformException` (surfaced as an error toast), rather than providing a clear recovery path. Added a reactive status badge to the habit editor and direct "Open Settings" actions.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - Updated `_pickAppsAndCategories` in `habit_management_modal.dart` to check if `status == ScreenTimeAuthorizationStatus.denied` and immediately abort/prompt the user, bypassing `requestIndividualAuthorization()` which throws an exception under these conditions due to the exclusive `.individual` iOS mutex.
    - Implemented a `_ScreenTimeAuthBadge` widget in the modal that reactively displays ✅ or ⚠️ along with an `openAppSettings()` action button using `permission_handler`.
    - Added an "Open Settings" button to the `ScreenTimeForm` in `screen_time_form.dart` when rendered in the `denied` state.
    - Added multi-language translation keys (`statusAuthorized`, `statusNotAuthorized`, `openSettings`) across all 5 `.i18n.json` files and regenerated `slang` files.
    - Added a step-by-step guide in `TO_SIMO_DO.md` for manually regenerating Xcode provisioning profiles to permanently resolve the Family Controls distribution entitlement revocation issue.

- [2026-07-17 21:44]: **Fix Privacy Mode Navigation Bug**
  - *Details*: Fixed a bug where returning from Privacy Mode to the login screen would unexpectedly drop the user into the Supabase Dashboard (or the Biometric Lock Gate) if a valid cached Supabase session existed.
  - *Tech Notes*: Modified `returnToLoginFromPrivateMode` in `auth_provider.dart` to explicitly call `supabase.auth.signOut()` before switching back to `AppDataMode.supabase`. This ensures `isLoggedIn` remains false, allowing `GoRouter` to properly route the user to `/login` as expected instead of auto-logging them into the cached session.

- [2026-07-17 16:14]: **Updated `.gitignore`**
  - *Details*: Added `node_modules/` and `dist/` to the repository root `.gitignore` to prevent generated dependency and build files from being tracked by git.
  - *Tech Notes*: Modified `.gitignore`.

  - *Tech Notes*: Removed `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`.

The message container now spans the full width of the panel, and individual chat bubbles have been widened for improved readability.
  - *Tech Notes*:

    - Increased the `maxWidth` constraint of individual `_MessageBubble` containers from 640px to 800px.

  - *Tech Notes*:
    - Added `statsHabitFilter` to `AppSettings` in `settings_provider.dart`, saving to SharedPreferences but excluded from Supabase sync to avoid DB migration.

- [2026-07-11]: **Apple-Style UI — Phase 2: kit expansion + app-wide migration (mobile, in progress)**
  - *Details*: Audited the whole mobile app (4 parallel read-only agents) → ~40 un-Apple surfaces that collapse into ~6 missing primitives. Built the primitive layer and migrated ~50 surfaces across 7 categories, primitive-first for full cross-platform coherence. Every batch verified: `flutter analyze` stays at 16 pre-existing issues (down from baseline 18; zero new), `flutter test` = 144 green. Visual QA is owner's. Full roadmap + resume state at `mobile/docs/apple-style-phase2-plan.md`.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - New primitives in `lib/ui/kit/`: `evolve_dialog.dart` (`showEvolveConfirm`/`showEvolveAlert` over `CupertinoAlertDialog`, app-brightness-aware, rich `content:` slot), `evolve_button.dart` (`EvolveButton` — filled/tinted/secondary/destructive/plain, luminance-contrast text, `haptic` param), `evolve_switch.dart` (`EvolveSwitch`/`EvolveSwitchRow` — CupertinoSwitch + gated haptic), `evolve_segmented_control.dart` (`EvolveSegmentedControl<T>`, equal-width), `evolve_section_header.dart` (13px muted, no forced uppercase), `evolve_toast.dart` (`showEvolveToast` — root-overlay fading banner). Barrel `evolve_kit.dart`.
    - Migrations: ~13 confirm/alert dialogs → showEvolveConfirm/Alert; 5 segmented pills → EvolveSegmentedControl; ~8 CTAs → EvolveButton; 4 settings switches → EvolveSwitch; section headers → EvolveSectionHeader (settings ×4 + the phase-1-deferred habit-modal field labels); 20 SnackBars → showEvolveToast; 5 selection sheets → showEvolveSheet (Pro-lock preserved).
    - Correctness fixes en route: ~10 ungated `HapticFeedback.*` → gated `ref.haptic*`; dead `dart:ui`/`services` imports removed; hardcoded English `'Close'` localized.
    - Deliberately preserved bespoke/brand: subscription success + `subscription_alert_modal` (hero/gradient), habit-modal gold Pro-upgrade CTA, dashboard onboarding CTAs, one-time tutorial coach-marks, consent legal-terms checkbox, `bottom_nav_bar`/chart CustomPaint.
    - Post-QA continuation (owner confirmed primitives clean): goal_item editor + ai-settings dialog → form sheets; accent-picker + 5 modal sheet chromes → EvolveGrabber; privacy delete/reset → showEvolveSheet; EvolveSpinner (paywall); `GoogleFonts.outfit`→inter; default-view trailing `.toUpperCase()` removed; 16/18 ungated haptics gated; day_details 22 static `AppColors`→`context.appColors` + `Icons.close`→Lucide. Final: `flutter analyze` = 16 (all pre-existing), `flutter test` = 144 green.
    - Test fix: `test/icloud_sync_screen_test.dart` `find.byType(Switch)`→`find.byType(CupertinoSwitch)` (matches the intentional T11 Material→Cupertino toggle migration). It went unnoticed because earlier gates piped `flutter test | tail` (masking the runner's exit code) — corrected to read the runner's own verdict.
    - Deferred as optional polish (owner happy with current UI): T15 settings-card grouped-inset restructure; privacy change-password editor; Cupertino time/date/detail pickers' nav-header chrome; 2 non-Consumer ungated haptics (protocollo tile, category editor) needing a widget-class refactor.

- [2026-07-11]: **Apple-Style UI Kit — implemented (mobile)**
  - *Details*: Built the shared `lib/ui/kit/` and migrated the three named surfaces to it so they read as native iOS: the "Select Habit" sheet (statistics), the "Choose category" sheet + its editor + delete confirm (goals), and every colour picker (habit, category, accent). Cross-platform (no `Platform.isIOS` gating), built on the existing `context.appColors`. Verified: `flutter analyze` clean (no new issues) and full suite green (144 tests). Visual QA is owner's.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - New `lib/ui/kit/evolve_sheet.dart`: `showEvolveSheet` (draggable selection sheet, grabber + 17pt centred title + detents 0.6→0.92), `showEvolveFormSheet` (keyboard-aware editor with Cancel/title/Done nav header), `EvolveListSection` (grouped-inset rounded card + hairline separators), `EvolveListRow` (ConsumerWidget → gated `hapticSelection`; accent `CupertinoIcons.check_mark` / chevron), `EvolveIconTile`, `EvolveColorDotTile`, `EvolveTextAction`.
    - New `lib/ui/kit/evolve_color_picker.dart`: `kEvolveDefaultPalette` (desktop's 6 colours), `EvolveColorSwatchGrid` (swatch circles + luminance-aware contrast + "Custom" cell; hooks `isLocked`/`onLockedTap`/`onCustomTap`/`customLocked` for the accent Pro-gate), `showEvolveColorPicker(context, initial)→Future<Color?>` (restyled `flutter_colorpicker` in a form sheet).
    - `statistics_screen.dart`: `_showGoalSelector` → `showEvolveSheet` (All-habits row + Pro-lock flow + selected highlight preserved).
    - `category_picker_sheet.dart`: rewritten — sheet → kit; editor `Dialog` → `showEvolveFormSheet`; delete `AlertDialog` → `CupertinoAlertDialog` (destructive). Post-dispose-safe notifier capture preserved.
    - `habit_management_modal.dart`: colour `Wrap` + Cupertino popup → `EvolveColorSwatchGrid`; removed local `_presetColors`/`_showColorPicker` + `flutter_colorpicker` import.
    - `app_settings_screen.dart`: accent presets → `EvolveColorSwatchGrid` with `premiumAccentColors` + `customLocked: !isPro` + `onCustomTap`; the `_showValidationDialog`/too-dark `SlidePicker` flow and light-mode white→dark remap kept intact.
    - Palette unification side effect: habit/category colours now default to the shared 6; any previously-saved off-palette colour still displays (rendered in the "Custom" cell), no data loss.
    - Deliberately out of scope (surgical): the habit modal's own `COLOR`/`REMINDER` micro-labels and the accent sheet's title/handle chrome were left as-is — only the colour control inside them was migrated.

- [2026-07-11]: **Apple-Style UI Kit — design spec finalized (mobile)**
  - *Details*: Agreed (via grill-me session) the full design/scope for making three mobile surfaces feel authentically Apple — "Select Habit" (statistics), the "Choose category" selector (goals), and every color picker — through one shared reusable kit. No code yet; this logs the finalized architectural decision. Full spec at `mobile/docs/apple-style-kit-spec.md`.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - New `lib/ui/kit/` (Cupertino-look on Material scaffolding, cross-platform, NOT `Platform.isIOS`-gated). `Evolve`-prefixed API mirroring desktop: `showEvolveSheet` / `showEvolveEditorSheet`, `EvolveListSection`, `EvolveListRow`, `EvolveColorSwatchGrid`, `showEvolveColorPicker`.
    - Sheet anatomy: grabber, bold 17pt centered title (kills the 10px uppercase label), solid `appColors.card`, `DraggableScrollableSheet` detents 0.6→0.92, grouped-inset rounded rows.
    - Color control: swatch-grid + "Custom" hatch; unified 6-color palette mirroring desktop (`kEvolveDefaultPalette`); accent site keeps premium Pro-gated palette via a per-cell locking hook; `flutter_colorpicker` retained but restyled.
    - Type/interaction: keep Inter (iOS scale), surgical `CupertinoIcons` checkmark+chevron, gated `Haptics.selectionClick`/`mediumImpact`, accent-tinted selection.
    - Blast radius: `statistics_screen.dart`, `macro_goals/category_picker_sheet.dart` (incl. editor→sheet, delete→`CupertinoAlertDialog`), `habit_management_modal.dart`, `app_settings_screen.dart`. Non-goals: no theme rename, no migration of the other ~12 sheets, no new deps.

- [2026-07-06 23:30]: **Cross-platform iCloud Sync (desktop macOS + shared engine extraction)**
    - *Details*: Implemented end-to-end-encrypted iCloud (CloudKit) sync on the macOS desktop app, wire-compatible with the existing iOS implementation — same container (`iCloud.com.simo.evolve`), zone (`PrivateZone`), record type (`PrivateRecord`), AES-256-GCM payload format and LWW/tombstone semantics — so all of a user's Apple devices converge on one private dataset. The entire sync core was extracted from `mobile/` into a new shared package **`packages/evolve_sync`** (engine, crypto, local store, bridge contract + MethodChannel impl, private-DB schema, sync service, key store, write debouncer, fakes + 73 tests) consumed by BOTH apps, making schema/wire-format drift between platforms impossible. Design record: `desktop/ICLOUD_SYNC_PLAN.md` (grill-me interview, 9 locked decisions).
    - *Tech Notes*:
      - **Key transport**: sync key + canonical owner moved to the shared keychain access group `$(AppIdentifierPrefix)com.simo.evolve.sync` (iCloud Keychain syncs across devices; only a shared access group crosses APPS). Mobile 1.0.10 migrates transparently via `MigratingSyncSecretStore` (read-heal, dual-write for ≤1.0.9 devices, delete-both); desktop reads the group directly (fss 10 `MacOsOptions`). Entitlements updated on both targets.
      - **New features on both platforms**: avatar sync as an encrypted `CKAsset` (`avatar:<owner>`, `SyncAvatarStore` seam, no-re-dirty re-localization via `SyncLocalStore.setLocalOnlyColumn`) and the after-write debounced trigger (`SyncWriteDebouncer`, ~3 s, hooked at the private write methods).
      - **Desktop wiring**: `DesktopSyncLifecycle` (launch + window-refocus + after-write + 15-min periodic triggers), `desktopPrivateSyncServiceProvider` (macOS-gated), `DesktopPrivateDb` gains syncStore/adoptOwner/avatar helpers + sync-aware `deleteAllPrivateData` (clears `sync_state`, preserves `pending_zone_wipe` — mobile #6/#7 parity). Native Swift `CloudKitSyncBridge` ported line-for-line into `desktop/macos/Runner/AppDelegate.swift`, registered in `MainFlutterWindow`; deployment target raised to macOS 12.3; `swiftc`-typechecked (no Xcode on this machine — compile + device QA delegated via TO_SIMO_DO.md).
      - **Desktop UI**: iCloud Sync card in Settings → Privacy (toggle + E2E disclosure, status incl. new `hasKey`-driven "waiting for iCloud Keychain" hint, Sync Now + last-synced); delete-private-data now performs the full sync reset (requestFullReset before local wipe) with the "run on each device" caveat. Strings in en/it/es/de/ar.
      - **Latent desktop bugs fixed en route**: private profile controller read/wrote non-existent `avatar_path` column (broke private profile load + avatar save); category archive didn't bump `updated_at` (LWW un-archive risk).
      - **Versions**: mobile bumped to 1.0.10+14 (Mac App Store release is gated on it being live). Suites: package 73/73, mobile 144/144, desktop 94/94; `flutter analyze` at baseline on all three.

- [2026-07-11]: **Cross-platform "swipe back" navigation (iOS edge-swipe + macOS two-finger trackpad)**
    - *Details*: Made "swipe to go back" work from every back-navigable page, aligning the gesture across platforms. On mobile, the 7 settings/detail sub-pages (Personal Info, Subscription, App Settings, Notifications, Privacy, App Logs, iCloud Sync) could not be swiped back because each pushed itself with a custom `PageRouteBuilder` (a hand-rolled 400 ms slide), which does not wire up Flutter's iOS edge-swipe-back gesture — only the Profile ("settings") screen and AI Chat worked, since they use `MaterialPageRoute`. All 7 now use `MaterialPageRoute`, so iOS gets the native Cupertino slide + edge-swipe-back for free (RTL-aware) and Android keeps its native Material transition; every call site (`XxxScreen.route()`) is unchanged. On macOS the app has no page stack (a sidebar shell of 6 peer sections; settings is a single page), so "back" was given a concrete meaning: `NavigationController` now keeps a visited-section history plus a `back()`/`canGoBack`/`lastDirection` API; a two-finger trackpad swipe-right and ⌘[ both return to the previously visited section, and the shell's existing section `AnimatedSwitcher` became a directional slide+fade driven by the navigation direction.
    - *Tech Notes*:
      - **Mobile**: 7 files under `mobile/lib/ui/screens/*` had their `static Route route()` body changed from `PageRouteBuilder` → `MaterialPageRoute` (`icloud_sync` keeps `<void>`); no import or behaviour changes, custom leading chevrons retained.
      - **Desktop `navigation_controller.dart`**: added `enum NavDirection { forward, back }`, a capped (50) `_history` stack; `select()` now records the prior section (dedup consecutive) and sets the direction; new `back()` / `canGoBack` / `lastDirection`. State type stays `DesktopSection`, so existing `ref.watch` call sites are untouched.
      - **Desktop `desktop_shell.dart`**: added `import 'package:flutter/gestures.dart'`; ⌘[ (`bracketLeft`, meta) → `back`; wrapped the content area (sidebar excluded) in a `Listener` using `onPointerPanZoom*` (macOS trackpad pans arrive as pointer pan-zoom events) that pops history on a horizontal-dominant swipe past a 48 px threshold, once per gesture, RTL-aware; the section `AnimatedSwitcher` gained a directional slide (±0.06) + fade `transitionBuilder` (220 ms, easeOutCubic) keyed off `lastDirection`.
      - **No new dependencies** (all Flutter built-ins). `flutter analyze` clean on both apps. Runtime swipe QA on iOS/macOS delegated via `TO_SIMO_DO.md` (no Xcode on this machine); the macOS trackpad pan-sign assumption is called out there for on-device confirmation.

- [2026-07-11]: **macOS forward navigation (two-finger swipe-left + ⌘])**
    - *Details*: Completed the macOS back/forward pair. The two-finger trackpad swipe-back added earlier now has its complement: a swipe in the opposite direction (left) — and ⌘] — re-enters the section you just backed out of, browser/Finder-style. Forward is a no-op until you've gone back, and any fresh navigation (sidebar click, ⌘1-5/⌘,, command palette) clears the forward history, matching standard browser semantics. Desktop-only: mobile's back-swipe pops and destroys the page, so there is nothing to go forward to.
    - *Tech Notes*:
      - **`navigation_controller.dart`**: added a `_forward` stack + `canGoForward`; `back()` now pushes the current section onto `_forward` before popping `_history`; new `forward()` pops `_forward`, pushes current back onto `_history`, sets `NavDirection.forward`; `select()` clears `_forward`.
      - **`desktop_shell.dart`**: the trackpad handler (`_backSwipe*` → `_navSwipe*`) now resolves both directions — swipe-right past the 48 px threshold → `back()`, swipe-left → `forward()` — RTL-aware, one-shot per gesture, horizontal-dominance guarded; added ⌘] (`bracketRight`, meta) → `forward`. No transition changes: `forward()` reuses the existing `NavDirection.forward` slide, so it animates in from the trailing edge automatically. No visible button.
      - **No new dependencies.** `flutter analyze` clean. Runtime forward-swipe QA delegated via `TO_SIMO_DO.md`.

- [2026-07-11]: **Fix two iOS runtime crashes: AI Coach open + Settings→back-to-login (mobile)**
    - *Details*: Two on-device QA crashes fixed in the mobile app.
      **AI Coach crashes on open**: `AIChatScreen.initState()` seeded the greeting via `_addInitialMessages()`, which reads `context.t` — the slang translations `InheritedWidget`. Inherited-widget lookups are illegal during `initState`, so opening the AI Coach threw `dependOnInheritedWidgetOfExactType<InheritedLocaleData<AppLocale, Translations>>() … was called before _AIChatScreenState.initState() completed`. The seeding now runs from `didChangeDependencies()`, guarded with `if (_messages.isEmpty)` so it runs exactly once and never re-seeds on later theme/locale changes. Greeting text and message model unchanged; the delete-chat re-seed path is untouched.
      **Settings→back-to-login crash (toast overlay leak)**: `showEvolveToast`'s `_EvolveToast` drove its ~2s dwell with a non-cancellable `await Future<void>.delayed(duration)` and only removed its `OverlayEntry` via `onDismissed`. A toast still on-screen when the route tree was torn down on logout (`context.go('/login')`) left the pending delay alive and its inherited dependents (Theme/MediaQuery) dangling, tripping `InheritedElement.debugDeactivated: assert(_dependents.isEmpty)` (framework.dart:6268 → the reported `_dependents.isEmpty is not true`). Ported the sibling desktop kit's hardening: the dwell now runs on a `Timer` stored in state and `cancel()`ed in `dispose()`, and `showEvolveToast` guards `entry.remove()` behind a `bool removed` flag so it is never called twice / on an already-removed entry. Visual behaviour (fade+slide in, ~2s, fade out) is identical.
    - *Tech Notes*:
      - **`mobile/lib/ui/screens/ai_chat_screen.dart`**: removed the `initState` seeding; added `didChangeDependencies()` with `if (_messages.isEmpty) _addInitialMessages();`. No other `context.` inherited lookups occur in `initState`.
      - **`mobile/lib/ui/kit/evolve_toast.dart`**: `+import 'dart:async';`; `_run()` (Future.delayed) → `_show()`/`_dismiss()` on a `Timer? _dismissTimer` cancelled in `dispose()`; `showEvolveToast` wraps `onDismissed` in a one-shot `removed` guard.
      - **No new dependencies; no API/behaviour changes.** Verified against the exact repros with two new widget tests: `mobile/test/ai_chat_screen_test.dart` (pumps `AIChatScreen` in Private mode, asserts it builds + seeds the greeting — reproduced the `InheritedLocaleData` throw pre-fix) and `mobile/test/evolve_toast_teardown_test.dart` (shows a toast then tears the tree down mid-dwell, asserting no pending timer / exception — reproduced "A Timer is still pending even after the widget tree was disposed" pre-fix; a second case asserts normal self-dismiss).
      - `flutter analyze`: **16 issues** (all pre-existing infos; 0 errors/warnings — baseline unchanged). `flutter test`: **All tests passed!** at **147** (was 144; +3 new). On-device re-verification of the two repro flows delegated via `TO_SIMO_DO.md` (no Xcode on this machine).

- [2026-07-13 15:55]: **Auto-Verified Habits — design decisions + verification core (`packages/evolve_verification`)**
  - *Details*: Started a new feature — habits whose daily `done`/`missed` is set **automatically** from Apple data instead of manual check-in: e.g. "≥10k steps" (HealthKit) or "≤2h screen time" (Screen Time). Verification is inherently **iOS-only** (no public HealthKit or Screen Time API exists on macOS); macOS + web display synced verdicts read-only. This entry lands the first, fully device-free-testable slice: the pure-Dart reconcile/verdict core. Native bridges, the schema migration, the DeviceActivity extension, UI and notifications are later slices (see *Current Status*).
  - *Design decisions* (grill session, all ratified):
    - **D1** v1 ships both HealthKit + Screen Time; iOS verifies, macOS/web display only.
    - **D2** Screen Time = *total* device usage, binary threshold (empty `DeviceActivityEvent`; over⇒missed, interval-end-clean⇒done). Raw minutes are never readable by our code.
    - **D3** Lazy-on-foreground reconciliation is authoritative; background is best-effort notification latency only; all writes idempotent + recomputable.
    - **D4** Split representation: verdicts land in `goal_logs` as ordinary `done`/`missed` (zero streak/RPC/sync churn); the rule = nullable columns on `goals`; a local-only unsynced table holds the reconcile queue + provenance + couldn't-verify.
    - **D5** Nine curated templates (steps, exercise min, active energy, stand hours, distance, mindful min, sleep hours, workout, total screen time); defer per-app screen time + compound rules.
    - **D6** Local-current-day boundaries; honor `frequency_days`; 7-day bounded backfill (HealthKit re-queries, Screen Time import-only); couldn't-verify auto-settles + stops nagging after ~2 days.
    - **D7** Two Dart bridges (`HealthKitBridge` query-based, `ScreenTimeBridge` event-based) + pure-Dart `VerificationService`, in a new `packages/evolve_verification`; native impls in `mobile/`; Monitor extension only (no Report extension).
    - **D8** Full schema parity (Supabase + `evolve_sync` v3→v4 + both Goal models + web type + read-only badge); reuse `goal_logs.value` for the HealthKit measured number (null for Screen Time); dedicated mobile-only local bookkeeping store; device-local screen-time verdicts documented.
    - **D9** Just-in-time per-type auth; verifiable goals degrade to manual habits; manual entries win + freeze the day; revocation detected directly (Screen Time) / via couldn't-verify streak (HealthKit); soft Watch-data warnings.
    - **D10** Forward-only rule edits (no rule-versioning); late verdicts → bounded streak-tail recompute; verification toggles per goal; deletes deregister monitors; 20-activity cap surfaced.
    - **D11** Accountability-forward notifications: Screen-Time threshold-cross (real-time, from the extension) + couldn't-verify nudge ON; HealthKit celebration + failure summary opt-in.
    - **D12** Front-load the 2× family-controls entitlement requests; ship dark behind a feature flag; HealthKit may enable before Screen Time; raw data never leaves the device.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **New package `packages/evolve_verification`** (pure Dart, `flutter`-dep for `@immutable` only; no platform channels — native bridges live in `mobile/`). Mirrors `evolve_sync`'s split: contract + logic + fakes here, so it is fully unit-testable without a device. Barrel `evolve_verification.dart`; test doubles exported separately from `testing.dart`.
    - **Models**: `VerificationProvider`/`VerificationComparator`/`VerificationAggregation`/`VerificationUnit` (stable `wireName`s persisted to `goals`); `VerificationTemplate` + `VerificationCatalog` (the 9 v1 templates, each pinning provider/comparator/unit/aggregation/native identifier + threshold bounds); `VerificationRule` with `toColumns()`/`fromColumns()` (all-null ⇒ manual habit; partial ⇒ null, never a half-active rule); `DayVerdict`/`VerificationOutcome` (pending/pass/fail/couldNotVerify).
    - **Bridges**: `HealthKitBridge` (`dailyQuantity`→`double?`, null = no-data/ambiguous → never a false miss; `hasRecentData` Watch probe) and `ScreenTimeBridge` (`syncMonitoredGoals`, `drainSignals`, `ScreenTimeGoalSpec`/`ScreenTimeSignal`, `ScreenTimeMonitorLimitException`). In-memory fakes for both.
    - **`VerificationService`**: static pure decision tables `evaluateHealthDay` / `evaluateScreenTimeDay`, and `reconcile()` → a declarative `ReconcilePlan` (`LogWrite`s + `CouldNotVerifyEntry`s) the caller applies. Honors scheduled-days, 7-day backfill, forward-only `effectiveFrom`, manual-freeze, idempotency, and drains Screen Time signals once per pass.
    - **Adversarial review** (one agent) caught and we fixed three real `reconcile()` defects, each now regression-tested: (1) couldn't-verify was re-emitted for days already terminally logged (ephemeral Screen Time signals) → guard on existing terminal outcome; (2) `Duration(days:1)` date arithmetic drifted off local midnight across DST, breaking midnight-keyed lookups → switched to calendar-safe `DateTime(y,m,d±n)`; (3) a logged Screen Time `missed` could be flipped back to `done` by a late duplicate `stayedUnder` → over-limit is now permanent (HealthKit fail→pass on late data still allowed).
    - **Verification**: `flutter analyze` clean; `flutter test` **37/37 pass**. No new app dependencies; nothing wired into `mobile/`/`desktop/` yet (package is standalone).
  - *Current Status*: Slice 1 (verification core) **complete + verified**. **Immediate next step**: Slice 2 — additive schema migration for the rule columns on `goals` (`evolve_sync` v3→v4 with `onUpgrade` + `schema_drift`/`private_db_schema` tests, plus the Supabase migration and web `Goal` type), and the mobile-only local `verification_state` table. Then: native Swift bridges (typecheck-only here) + the `DeviceActivityMonitor` extension, `mobile/` wiring (Goal model + provider + reconcile-on-foreground), the creation/badge UI, notifications, and the feature flag. Manual (Xcode/Apple) actions logged in `TO_SIMO_DO.md`.

- [2026-07-13 17:20]: **Auto-Verified Habits — Slice 2: schema & coherence for the rule columns**
  - *Details*: Landed the `goals.verify_*` verification-rule columns across every backend and client so a rule persists and syncs without being wiped, per decision D8. No behavioural change yet (no verified goals can be created until the UI slice); this is the additive data-model foundation. The measured HealthKit number will reuse the pre-existing `goal_logs.value` column (no migration).
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **`evolve_sync` schema v3→v4** (`packages/evolve_sync/lib/src/private_db_schema.dart`): added five nullable columns to the `goals` DDL (`verify_provider`, `verify_metric`, `verify_comparator`, `verify_threshold REAL`, `verify_unit`) + an idempotent `_upgradeToV4` (`ALTER TABLE goals ADD COLUMN …`). Left **unconstrained** (no CHECK) so a future provider/metric from a newer client round-trips instead of being rejected. The columns flow through iCloud sync automatically — the sync engine serializes whole rows (`readRow` is `SELECT *`) and `applyUpsert` re-inserts the map; per-record try/catch in the apply loop means a mixed-version device just logs+retries a not-yet-appliable row rather than corrupting data. Both apps ship `evolve_sync` together, so the mixed-version window is bounded. New tests: fresh-schema column presence + a v3→v4 migration test (columns added, existing rows preserved, rule writes/reads back). `flutter test` **75/75**.
    - **Supabase / web**: added the same columns to `schema.sql` `public.goals` + a new `migrations/20260713_add_goal_verification_columns.sql` (`ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`, numeric threshold, column comments, no RLS change). Web `src/types/goals.ts` `Goal` gained the optional `verify_*` fields + `VerificationProvider`/`VerificationComparator` unions (additive-optional; can't break the TS build). **Manual step (logged in TO_SIMO_DO):** apply the migration to the live Supabase project.
    - **Goal models & every read/write path** (both apps depend on the new `evolve_verification` package): mobile `Goal` and desktop `DashboardHabit` gained a `VerificationRule? verificationRule` field (via `VerificationRule.fromColumns`/`toColumns`), threaded through `copyWith`, JSON, and the DB row-mappers. Critical wipe-guard: mobile `upsertGoal` and the sync `applyUpsert` use `ConflictAlgorithm.replace`, so the private row-mappers (`_goalToRow`, desktop `createHabit`/`updateHabit`) now **always** write the columns (null when manual) — an omitted column on REPLACE would NULL a synced rule. The **Supabase-facing** serializers (`Goal.toJson`, `DashboardHabit.toRemoteJson`) include `verify_*` **only when a rule is present**, so manual-habit cloud writes don't depend on the Supabase migration having been applied yet. Reads (`_goalFromRow`, `_habitFromRow`, `fromRemoteJson`) parse the columns back.
    - *Deferred within this feature* (no data at risk — no verified goals exist until the UI slice): backup export/import round-trip of `verify_*` (mobile `import_merge`/`backup_import_service` + desktop `_goalRow`) lands with the creation UI.
  - *Verification*: `flutter analyze` — mobile **0 errors/warnings** (17 pre-existing infos; regenerated stale `translations.g.dart` via `dart run slang`), desktop **0 errors** (1 pre-existing `main.dart` warning). Tests: `evolve_verification` 37/37, `evolve_sync` 75/75, **mobile 147/147**, desktop 143 pass / **2 pre-existing failures** (`import_merge_lww` `goal_logs.value` round-trip + an `icloud_sync_card` widget test) — **confirmed pre-existing** by running them in an isolated worktree at HEAD (they fail identically without any Slice 2 change). No new dependencies beyond the `evolve_verification` path package wired into both apps.
  - *Current Status*: Slice 2 **complete + verified** (no regressions; 2 unrelated pre-existing desktop failures flagged separately). **Immediate next step**: Slice 3 — the mobile-only local `verification_state` bookkeeping store + the `VerificationService` wiring into a mobile provider (reconcile-on-foreground → apply `ReconcilePlan` to `goal_logs` with streak-tail recompute), all still testable here; then the native Swift bridges (`HealthKitBridge`/`ScreenTimeBridge` MethodChannel impls, Swift typecheck-only on this machine) + the `DeviceActivityMonitor` extension, behind the feature flag.

- **2026-07-13**: Fix — Biometric (Face ID / Touch ID) app lock never engaged
  - *Details*: A deep multi-agent audit found the lock was dead by construction. In `dashboard_screen.dart` the gate flag `_isBiometricAuthenticated` was initialised `true` and never set `false` anywhere, so the lock branch (`isLocked && !_isBiometricAuthenticated`) and the dashboard `_authenticate()` prompt were unreachable — enabling the toggle changed nothing at runtime (the user's report: "behaviour doesn't change when it's on or off"). There was also no lifecycle re-lock, no root-level gate, a private-mode first-frame race (biometricLock defaulted false while the SQLite settings row loaded async), and a Supabase pull that let a stale/other-device `biometric_lock=false` clobber a locally-enabled lock.
  - *Fix*: Introduced an app-wide `BiometricLockGate` (`mobile/lib/ui/widgets/biometric_lock_gate.dart`) mounted in `MaterialApp.router`'s `builder`, so it wraps every route and keeps the app mounted beneath an opaque overlay (navigation state survives lock/unlock). It owns the session auth via `biometricUnlockedProvider` (Notifier<bool>) + derived `biometricLockEnabledForUserProvider` / `biometricLockActiveProvider`; is a `WidgetsBindingObserver` that re-arms on `paused` and prompts on `resumed`; covers content while not-`resumed` (app-switcher privacy); and fails **closed** on auth error/cancel but **open** only when no biometrics are enrolled (avoids permanent lockout). Removed the dead lock code from `dashboard_screen.dart` and deferred its onboarding/tutorial until the lock clears. Privacy settings marks the session unlocked when enabling (it already did Face ID) to avoid an immediate re-prompt. `settings_provider.dart`: seed `biometricLock` synchronously from SharedPreferences on the private-mode first frame (+ mirror on save/load), and keep the local value authoritative on Supabase pull.
  - *Tech Notes*: No new dependencies (`local_auth: ^3.0.1` already present; API `authenticate(localizedReason, biometricOnly, persistAcrossBackgrounding)` confirmed valid). New providers: `biometricUnlockedProvider`, `biometricLockEnabledForUserProvider`, `biometricLockActiveProvider`. Files: +`ui/widgets/biometric_lock_gate.dart`, +`test/biometric_lock_gate_test.dart` (3 tests), edited `main.dart`, `providers/settings_provider.dart`, `ui/screens/dashboard_screen.dart`, `ui/screens/privacy_settings_screen.dart`. `flutter analyze` clean (no new issues); `flutter test` **160/160 pass**. Manual device verification + iOS SPM plugin-wiring check logged in `TO_SIMO_DO.md`.
