- [2026-07-23]: **macOS (desktop) Evolve Pro paywall → production parity with mobile**
  - *Details*: Brought the macOS purchase surface to functional + compliance parity with the live iOS paywall, after a full `/grill-me` pass. Locked decisions: native macOS presentation (the existing `showPaywallDialog` modal + Settings→Subscription section, both rendering the single `_SubscriptionSettings`), **identical core purchase logic and prices** to mobile; Manage/Cancel keeps the external Apple page (`apps.apple.com/account/subscriptions`) — RevenueCat's in-app Customer Center is iOS/Android-only, so no `purchases_ui_flutter` dependency was added; a single "Manage subscription" action (not a redundant Cancel that opens the same page); Manage shown only in the already-Pro view; outcome feedback via `EvolveToast` + the celebratory success dialog. No RevenueCat dashboard work required — macOS bundle id is `com.simo.evolve`, identical to iOS, so Universal Purchase shares the same app/products/entitlement/Offering and the `appl_` key (verified against RevenueCat's Legacy-Mac docs).
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **`desktop_subscription_controller.dart`** — ported the mobile `subscription_service.dart` logic: (1) **two-tier price resolution** in `refresh()` — `Purchases.getOfferings().current` then a direct `Purchases.getProducts([monthly,yearly])` fallback so prices/savings still render before an Offering is published; the offering fetch is caught locally so it can't blank the surface, and `getCustomerInfo()` is fetched last & unguarded so a store failure leaves the **offline-first seeded `isPro` untouched** (the invariant `subscription_entitlement_scope_test` pins). (2) `DesktopSubscriptionState` gained `monthlyProduct`/`yearlyProduct` (`StoreProduct`) + `customerInfo`; the `CustomerInfoUpdateListener` now also captures `customerInfo`. (3) `purchase()`/`restore()` return typed results (`DesktopPurchaseResult`/`DesktopRestoreResult` with status enums) instead of `bool`+inline `message` — silent user-cancel, `pending`/deferred, `registeredNotActive`, already-purchased→auto-restore. (4) Full `PurchasesErrorCode`→localized-copy mapping (`_purchaseErrorMessage`/`_restoreErrorMessage`), paid-apps-agreement text match, and a `proDetails()` presentation snapshot (`DesktopProDetails`) so the UI needs no `purchases_flutter` types. `manageSubscription()` now returns `bool`.
    - **`settings_page.dart` `_SubscriptionSettings`** — split into a **not-Pro purchase view** (feature pitch [modal-suppressed] · plan cards priced from the resolved `StoreProduct`s · **honest annual per-month + saving** via the new top-level `annualSavingPercent()` + `pricePerMonthString`, falling back to the neutral "best value" when no price resolves · compliance links · Activate + Restore) and an **already-Pro details view** (status header · `detailsHeader` group with plan / Active / next-renewal-or-expiry date [`intl` `DateFormat`, localized] / payment method · Manage + Restore). Purchase success shows the success dialog and, in the modal, closes it via a new `onProActivated` callback so the user lands back on the unlocked feature. Restore/error outcomes route through `showEvolveToast`; user-cancel is silent.
    - **i18n** — ported mobile's already-translated subscription strings into desktop across **all 5 locales** (en/it/es/de/ar): 13 `settingsPage` keys (per-month/savings with `{price}`/`{percent}`, status/details rows) + 16 `subscriptionCtrl` keys (the error map + restore statuses). Three controller messages (`invalidConfig`, `loadOffersFailed`, `configKey`) were deliberately **reworded to drop vendor/SDK names** (desktop's `subscription_compliance_test` bans user-visible "revenuecat"/"storekit"). `dart run slang` re-run from the `desktop/` root. Added `intl: ^0.20.2` as a direct dependency.
    - **Tests** — new `subscription_saving_percent_test.dart` (8 cases, ported from mobile's identical guard); updated the pre-existing **stale** `pro_features_modal_test` (its CTA asserts the paywall opens in place, not the old deep-link-to-Settings that the recorded design already removed); updated `widget_test` to reflect Manage now being Pro-only. Verified headless: `flutter analyze` — 0 new issues (4 pre-existing, unrelated); `flutter test` — **525 passing**, the single remaining failure (`coach_settings_widget_test`) is pre-existing on `main` and unrelated to this change.
    - **Adversarial review pass** (multi-agent, over correctness/parity/compliance/i18n) surfaced 2 confirmed edge-case defects, both fixed: (1) the already-purchased→auto-restore branch mapped a *cancelled* restore prompt and a not-yet-propagated entitlement to a hard "purchase failed" error toast — now mapped to silent-cancel and the benign "registered, not active" warning respectively, matching the direct path + mobile; (2) `refresh()` committed resolved prices only *after* the unguarded `getCustomerInfo()`, so a CustomerInfo failure discarded freshly-fetched prices — prices are now committed in a separate `copyWith` first (isPro still left to the entitlement fetch, preserving the offline-first invariant). Compliance + i18n dimensions came back clean.
  - *Current Status*: Implemented and verified headless (analyze + full test suite; no Xcode on this machine). **Needs on-device StoreKit-sandbox QA on the Mac mini** — see `TO_SIMO_DO.md`: real purchase (monthly + annual), restore, cancel-the-sheet (must stay silent), the already-Pro details panel with a live renewal date, and Manage opening Apple's page.

- [2026-07-23]: **Desktop Habits › Protocol tab — show only active habits** (after a full `/grill-me` pass)
  - *Details*: The Protocol table previously rendered `snapshot.habits` **unfiltered**, so ended and not-yet-started habits stayed in the list. It now shows only habits active *today*, using the **same rule as the Statistics › Habits "Active" filter** (`DashboardHabit.isActiveOn(now)`: today falls inside `[startDate, endDate]` and the `isActive` flag is set). Locked decisions from the grill: (1) **always-on hard filter**, no per-tab toggle (unlike Statistics, which keeps its Active/All switch); (2) the rule is **range-based, not schedule-based** — an off-schedule-today habit (e.g. Mon/Wed/Fri viewed on Sunday) **still shows**, because the table's columns span the whole week (`isScheduledOn` would wrongly hide it); (3) the "Active protocol" **summary card is left untouched** — it counts `isScheduledOn(now)` and feeds today's completion math (`totalHabits`→`completionRate`), so retargeting it would corrupt the "X of Y done today" denominator; (4) the **Calendar tab is unchanged** (already filters per-day via `isScheduledOn`); (5) the empty state **reuses the existing** `_EmptyProtocol` CTA for both "no habits" and "no active habits". A brand-new desktop habit is created with `startDate: DateTime.now()`, so it is active immediately and never hidden by the filter.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **`habits_page.dart` `_ProtocolPanel`** — the displayed list is now `snapshot.habits.where((h) => h.isActiveOn(DateTime.now()))`; the tour override (view-only demo row) still bypasses the filter, and the demo habit is active anyway.
    - **Reorder correctness (the subtle part)** — drag-to-reorder feeds `ReorderableListView` indices that index the *rendered* list; once the list is filtered, those no longer match `state.habits`. A naive "map to full index + `reorderHabits(oldIndex,newIndex)`" is wrong: its remove-then-insert drags an inactive habit sitting *between* two reordered active rows along with the move. Fixed with **positional substitution**: a new pure helper `reorderActiveHabits()` (`lib/features/habits/application/protocol_reorder.dart`) recomputes the visible order, then refills only the *active slots* of the full list in that new order — every inactive habit keeps its exact position. The panel hands the rebuilt full list to a **new controller method `reorderHabitsList(List<DashboardHabit>)`** (reassigns `displayOrder` 0..n-1, optimistic + local-first + `_syncRemote`, identical persistence to the old path); the existing `reorderHabits(int,int)` now delegates to it, keeping its test green. `_ProtocolPanel.onReorder` retyped `void Function(int,int)` → `ValueChanged<List<DashboardHabit>>`.
    - **Tests** — new `test/protocol_reorder_test.dart` (6 cases): active-subset reorder pins an inactive habit in place, not-yet-started + `isActive:false` both treated as hidden, all-active behaves like a plain reorder, and no-op / out-of-range return the same list identity. The pre-existing `dashboard_controller_test` reorder case still passes through the delegated path.
    - No i18n changes, no new dependencies, no schema/RPC changes. Filter is purely presentational — streaks, reminders, best-streak and completion math still operate on the full list in the controller.
    - Verified headless: `flutter analyze` on the changed files — **0 issues** (3 pre-existing lint nits elsewhere in `lib/`, unrelated); new + delegated reorder tests **green**. The only consistent full-suite failure remains `coach_settings_widget_test` — pre-existing on `main`, unrelated (confirmed: it references none of the changed files).
  - *Current Status*: Implemented and verified headless (no Xcode on this machine). **Needs a quick on-device visual QA on the Mac mini** — see `TO_SIMO_DO.md`: confirm ended/upcoming habits disappear from Protocol, an off-schedule-today active habit still shows, and drag-reorder still lands the right habit while any hidden habits keep their order.

- [2026-07-23]: **Auto-verified habits — D10 forward-only rule-edit freezing (Slice 1: schema + migration)**
  - *Details*: Editing a verification rule (e.g. a step-goal threshold) re-derived the whole 7-day backfill window under the new value, silently rewriting recent history — because `effectiveFrom` was hard-wired to the goal `start_date` (`verification_wiring.dart` documented this as deferred: "forward-only rule edits would move this once edit-dates are tracked, D10"). This slice lays the storage foundation: a new nullable `goals.verify_effective_from` column (the day the current rule took effect). Later slices thread it through the models, the save-time stamp, and the reconcile wiring. The reconcile ENGINE already freezes days before `effectiveFrom` (it iterates from `max(windowStart, effectiveFrom)`), so no engine change is needed — only feeding it a real edit-date instead of `start_date`.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **`packages/evolve_sync/lib/src/private_db_schema.dart`** — schema `version` **6 → 7**; new idempotent `_upgradeToV7` (PRAGMA-guarded `ALTER TABLE goals ADD COLUMN verify_effective_from TEXT`, mirroring `_upgradeToV4`) + ladder entry `if (oldVersion < 7)`; column added to the `CREATE TABLE goals` DDL for fresh installs; version doc comment extended.
    - **`migrations/20260723_add_goal_verify_effective_from.sql`** (NEW) — the Supabase side: additive nullable `verify_effective_from date` + column comment, no RLS change. Manual apply logged in `TO_SIMO_DO.md`.
    - Semantics (fixed for later slices): NULL ⇒ fall back to `start_date` (upgraded DBs keep current behavior until the rule is next edited — no surprise re-freezing); the client will stamp "today" on rule create and on any change to the rule’s verifiable content, preserved across non-rule edits (title/color/reminder/schedule).
    - **Tests**: new `packages/evolve_sync/test/private_db_schema_v7_effective_from_test.dart` (3 cases: migration lands the column on a v6-shape table; re-running the upgrade is idempotent — no duplicate-column throw; a fresh install already has the column). Verified headless: `flutter analyze` **0 issues**; full `evolve_sync` suite **200 pass** (+3 new).
  - *Current Status*: Slice 1 of 6 DONE + verified headless. **Next = Slice 2 (models)**: add `verifyEffectiveFrom` to the mobile + desktop `Goal` models (field, `copyWith`+clear, `toJson`/`fromJson`) and the web `goals.ts` type.


- [2026-07-23]: **D10 forward-only rule-edit freezing — COMPLETE (Slices 2–6)**
  - *Details*: Threaded the `verify_effective_from` anchor through the models, the save-time stamp, the reconcile wiring, and the full backup/import round-trip on both apps. Editing a verification rule now takes effect from the edit day forward instead of silently re-deriving the last 7 days. Verification stays iOS-only; desktop/web carry the column so a cross-client edit can't wipe it.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **Models**: mobile `Goal` + desktop `DashboardHabit` gained `verifyEffectiveFrom` (field, copyWith+clear, toJson/fromJson, date-only `YYYY-MM-DD`); web `goals.ts` optional `verify_effective_from`.
    - **Save stamp**: pure `stampVerificationEffectiveFrom(updated, previous, today)` in `goal.dart` — stamps today when the rule is newly set or its `VerificationRule` value-equality changed; preserves the prior anchor (incl. null) on a non-rule edit (title/colour/schedule); clears it for a manual habit. Called at the top of `addHabit`/`updateHabit`; Supabase path explicit-nulls it on rule clear.
    - **Persistence + wiring**: mobile `_goalFromRow`/`_goalToRow` read/write it explicitly (upsert is ConflictAlgorithm.replace, so an omitted column would wipe it); `verifiableGoalsFrom` now sets `effectiveFrom = max(startDate, verifyEffectiveFrom)` — the actual freeze. The reconcile ENGINE was already correct (iterates from `effectiveFrom`); only the fed date changed.
    - **Backup**: mobile export + import (both normalize branches, validate, insert `_goalRow`); desktop export + `import_merge` (validate + row) + `applyImport` `_goalRow` + `desktop_backup_import_service`; the desktop repository READ now populates `verifyEffectiveFrom` (else a desktop edit would have wiped the anchor).
    - **Tests**: mobile — new `verify_effective_from_stamp_test.dart` (11: stamp matrix + JSON round-trip), `verification_wiring_test.dart` (+3 freeze-anchor), `import_merge_test.dart` (+1 round-trip assert). evolve_sync — new `private_db_schema_v7_effective_from_test.dart` (3: migration lands column / idempotent / fresh install). desktop — `backup_roundtrip_test.dart` (+ verified-habit anchor round-trip). Full suites: **evolve_sync 203**, **mobile 471**, **desktop 542** pass (+1 pre-existing Supabase dart-defines env fail). `flutter analyze`: no new issues on any changed file.
  - *Current Status*: **COMPLETE + verified headless** (pure Dart/SQL/TS — no native/Xcode surface). Outstanding manual step: apply the Supabase migration (see TO_SIMO_DO.md). Compound verifiable habits (OR/AND) remain fully DESIGNED (Q1–Q10) but NOT implemented — a separate follow-on that adds a `verify_conditions` column on top of this.


- [2026-07-23]: **Compound verifiable habits — OR/AND (2–3 HealthKit conditions), ships DARK**
  - *Details*: Combine 2–3 HealthKit conditions into one auto-verified habit ("10k steps OR 30 min exercise"), with OR (pass if any) or AND (pass if all). HealthKit-only for v1 (Q2) but the storage model is provider-general so cross-provider is later-additive. Verification stays iOS-only; desktop shows it read-only; both apps + web carry the data losslessly. Gated behind `VerificationConfig.compoundVerificationEnabled` (false) — flip after on-device QA.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **Core (`packages/evolve_verification`)**: new `VerificationJoin{or,and}`; `VerificationRule.toWire/fromWire`; `verification_conditions.dart` (`VerificationConditions`, `encode/decodeVerifyConditions` with a `{v,op,conditions}` envelope + strict non-throwing decode, `verificationColumnsFor`/`readVerificationColumns` for the flat-vs-JSON column bridge, `kMaxVerificationConditions=3`); `VerifiableGoal` gained `additionalConditions`+`join`+`conditions`/`isCompound`; `VerificationService.combineVerdicts` (Q5 truth table — OR: pass>pending>couldNotVerify>fail, AND: fail>couldNotVerify>pending>pass, reduces to the single verdict at N=1, drops the scalar value for compound per Q6); reconcile added an `isCompound` branch (pull each HealthKit condition, combine) — Screen Time permanence stays inert, D10 freeze still bounds the loop.
    - **Storage (Q4)**: new nullable `goals.verify_conditions` TEXT (evolve_sync **v7→v8** `_upgradeToV8` + `migrations/20260723_add_goal_verify_conditions.sql`, manual apply). A single rule stays on the flat `verify_*` columns; a compound writes `verify_conditions` and NULLS the flat columns (a pre-compound client reads it as manual — never mis-verifies). Read precedence: verify_conditions wins if valid, else flat, else manual.
    - **Models**: mobile `Goal` + desktop `DashboardHabit` gained `additionalConditions`+`verificationJoin` (verificationRule = the FIRST condition, so all existing "verified" checks still work); read via `readVerificationColumns`, write via `verificationColumnsFor`. `stampVerificationEffectiveFrom` (D10) now re-stamps on ANY condition/operator change via `Goal.sameVerificationAs`.
    - **Save + wiring**: `goal_provider` threads `additionalConditions`/`verificationJoin` (create+edit) with a `hasCompound` gate and a Supabase clear-path that nulls verify_conditions; `verifiableGoalsFrom` skips a compound goal unless BOTH `compoundEnabled` AND `healthKitEnabled` and builds the compound `VerifiableGoal`.
    - **UI (Q8)**: `VerificationRuleField` gained `showSwitch`; new `CompoundConditionsField` — plain-language "Any of these / All of these" toggle, removable condition rows, Pro-gated "+ Add condition" (→ paywall) capped at 3, editing each via a bottom sheet reusing the picker+stepper. Wired into `habit_management_modal` (state, save, init-from-habit, resets, primary-metric-change dedup). i18n `verification.compound.*` across all 5 locales (ar machine-MSA, flagged).
    - **Tests**: evolve_verification **88** (combinator truth table, conditions codec + column helpers, reconcile-compound OR/AND); evolve_sync **206** (+v8 migration); mobile **488** (Goal compound serialization + re-stamp, wiring gate, import round-trip, CompoundConditionsField widget test); desktop **543** (+compound backup round-trip; 1 pre-existing Supabase dart-defines env fail). `flutter analyze` clean on every changed file across all four packages. D10 freezing independently re-reviewed (6-dimension adversarial workflow, 0 findings).
  - *Current Status*: **FUNCTIONALLY COMPLETE + verified headless, DARK.** Remaining: apply the Supabase migration, native Arabic review, on-device QA → flip `compoundVerificationEnabled` (see TO_SIMO_DO.md). No native/Xcode work (HealthKit-only, reuses existing bridges).


- [2026-07-23]: **Compound habits — adversarial review pass (3 high-sev bugs fixed)**
  - *Details*: A 6-dimension adversarial-review workflow over the compound implementation confirmed 3 high-severity defects (the other 4 dimensions clean). All fixed + regression-tested.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - **Decode never throws (findings 1–2)**: `decodeVerifyConditions` cast `map['op'] as String?` OUTSIDE its try/catch, and `VerificationRule.fromWire` cast fields with `as String?`/`as num?`; a corrupt/foreign/newer-client `verify_conditions` blob with a wrong-typed `op` or condition field threw an uncaught `TypeError` that propagated out through `readVerificationColumns` → `Goal.fromJson` — which decodes goals via an eager `rows.map(...).toList()`, so ONE bad row would crash the whole goal list (hiding every habit) instead of degrading that habit to manual. Fixed: `fromWire` now uses `is`-checked reads; `decodeVerifyConditions` reads `op` type-safely AND wraps the whole body in try/catch → null. New tests cover numeric/bool/object `op` and wrong-typed condition fields.
    - **Cloud import kept the rule (finding 3)**: mobile `planCloudImport` (account/Supabase-mode restore) omitted ALL verification columns (pre-existing — even the flat `verify_*`), so importing a verified habit into an account silently stripped auto-verification. Now carries `verify_provider/metric/comparator/threshold/unit` + `verify_effective_from` + `verify_conditions`, matching the desktop cloud plan, `Goal.toJson`, and the private `_goalRow`. Regression test in `cloud_import_plan_test.dart`.
    - Post-fix: evolve_verification **90**, mobile **489**, desktop **543** (+1 pre-existing env fail), evolve_sync 206; analyze clean.

- [2026-07-24]: **Unified Home back/forward animation across Month / Week / Year tabs**
  - *Details*: The three Home (`HomeScreen`) calendar tabs used two different prev/next transitions — Month had the "premium parallax" slide (translate + scale-down + cross-fade on an infinite `PageView`), while Week and Year used a 3D `rotateY` cube-flip (`AnimatedSwitcher`) driven by a discrete velocity-threshold swipe. Week and Year now use the exact same parallax animation and the same finger-follows-drag interaction as Month. Each view keeps its own content and layout (Week's radar chart + summary, Year's 12-month density grid, Month's day grid) and its own header/nav-button styling — only the transition + swipe interaction changed. Added Month's tap-title-to-return-to-today shortcut to Week (tap the date range) and Year (tap the year) for full parity, and unified Year's nav haptic from `hapticLight` to `hapticAction` to match Month/Week.
  - *Tech Notes*: New shared widget `lib/ui/widgets/infinite_period_pager.dart` (`InfinitePeriodPager` + `PeriodPagerActions`) is now the single source of truth for the animation — it owns the `PageController` (basePage 1200), the infinite `PageView.builder`, the parallax `AnimatedBuilder` (`translation = pageOffset*60`, `scale = 1 − (absOffset*0.05).clamp(0,0.05)`, `opacity = 1 − absOffset`) and the nav actions (prev/next = `nextPage/previousPage` 400ms `easeOutCubic`; today = `animateToPage(basePage)` 500ms `easeOutQuart`). `habit_calendar_widget.dart` (Month), `weekly_view_widget.dart` (Week) and `yearly_view_widget.dart` (Year) were refactored to build their per-period content via the pager's `pageBuilder(context, pageDelta, actions)` and no longer own a `PageController`/`AnimatedSwitcher`/swipe `GestureDetector`. Removed the now-dead `_currentWeekStart`/`_currentYear`/`_slideDirection` local state and the `dart:math` flip import from Week/Year. No new dependencies, no API/schema changes. `flutter analyze` clean on all four files; full suite green (567 tests); `test/yearly_view_repaint_test.dart` unchanged and passing (the new `PageView` builds only the current page at rest, so still exactly 12 `_MonthBarsPainter`s). Goals (`macro_goals_screen.dart`) still carries its own copy of the parallax pattern — candidate fast-follow to adopt `InfinitePeriodPager`. On-device QA pending (no Xcode/simulator on this Mac).


- [2026-07-24]: **Mobile manage-habits list — verified indicator moved to a quiet second row**
  - *Details*: In the "Manage habits" reorder list, an auto-verified habit's full-text badge ("Verificato automaticamente") shared the row with the name (in `Expanded`) and the edit/delete buttons. On a narrow phone the long localized string starved the name column, wrapping the title one letter per line (reported via screenshot). Moved the indicator onto a second line under the name so the title keeps the full row-1 width.
  - *Tech Notes*:
    - `mobile/lib/ui/widgets/habit_management_modal.dart` — `_HabitListItem` only. The name `Text` is now wrapped in a left-aligned `Column` (`mainAxisSize.min`) and gains `maxLines: 1` + `TextOverflow.ellipsis`, which structurally prevents the letter-per-line collapse from recurring even if trailing controls grow. Verified habits render a second line: `LucideIcons.shieldCheck` (size 12, `colorScheme.primary`) + muted `verification.autoVerified` text (11px, `mutedForeground`), **no pill** — a plainer treatment than the shared `VerificationBadge`, matching the existing `day_details_modal` subtitle style. The old trailing inline `VerificationBadge` block was removed from the outer row; leading dot and trailing edit/delete stay vertically centered.
    - Reuses the existing `verification.autoVerified` string (no new i18n). No new deps, no schema/migration, no manual steps. Scope: mobile only — desktop (wide window, separate `VerifiedHabitBadge`) and `day_details_modal` (badge inline on row 1, name `Flexible`) don't exhibit the crush, and the shared `VerificationBadge` is left untouched.
    - *Verify*: `flutter analyze lib/ui/widgets/habit_management_modal.dart` → "No issues found!".

- [2026-07-24]: Habit classes — Phase 1: target_effective_from (v11 forward-only target freeze)
  - *Details*: Added a forward-only "effective-from" anchor for quantitative habit targets — the exact analogue of `verify_effective_from` (D10) for verification rules. Editing a target's amount, or switching a habit's tracking class, now applies FORWARD only: the manual-target end-of-day sweep never re-derives past `goal_logs` days. Fixes the retroactive-rewrite bug the pre-flip adversarial audit confirmed (raising an `atLeast` target flipped past `done`→`missed`; a Checkbox→limit change flipped past unlogged days to `done`). The targets UI remains dark; this is a domain-layer correctness fix that must land before the flag flips.
  - *Tech Notes*: Private schema `evolve_sync` v10→**v11** (`_upgradeToV11` = idempotent `ALTER TABLE goals ADD COLUMN target_effective_from TEXT`; onCreate DDL parity). Supabase migration `migrations/20260724_add_goal_target_effective_from.sql` (additive nullable — MANUAL APPLY pending, see TO_SIMO_DO.md). New `Goal.targetEffectiveFrom` + pure `stampTargetEffectiveFrom(updated,{previous,today})` (mobile `goal.dart`) and `DashboardHabit.targetEffectiveFrom` + top-level `stampTargetEffectiveFrom` (desktop `dashboard_models.dart`), stamped centrally in `addHabit`/`updateHabit` on both apps (never by the creation UI). `reconcileManualTargetDays` gained an `effectiveFrom` param clamping the sweep window to `max(start, anchor)`; both call sites (mobile `main.dart` resume hook, desktop `refresh` tail) pass it. Round-trips through BOTH sync modes (private DB read/write + whole-row CloudKit sync; Account-mode `toJson`/`fromJson` + `toRemoteJson`/`fromJson`) and every backup/import allowlist on both apps. Tests: evolve_sync 235, evolve_targets 107, mobile 577, desktop 588 — all green (+ v11 migration test, + target-stamp test, + 4 reconcile freeze tests). No new deps; no native/Xcode work.

- [2026-07-24]: Habit classes — Phase 0: pre-flip audit fixes (both-flags-on correctness + cloud backup + migration gating)
  - *Details*: An adversarial multi-agent audit of the built (dark) compound + quantitative-targets code, run before flipping any flag, confirmed 9 real defects that surface the moment `TargetsConfig` goes live. Fixed the 5 blocking/high ones (the 4 freeze findings are resolved by Phase 1's `target_effective_from`; 1 was refuted as unreachable):
    - **Owner arbitration (2 blockers).** A habit carrying BOTH a manual target and a verification rule was claimed by two independent reconcile pipelines writing the same `goal_logs` row → the day's status flip-flopped on every foreground, and a single manual-stepper tap deleted a HealthKit-earned `done` and zeroed the streak. Fix: one owner of `goal_logs.status` per habit-day — a verified habit is owned by the verification pipeline; the manual end-of-day sweep and manual `setProgress`/`setHabitProgressForDay` now skip it on both apps (defense-in-depth; the class picker also keeps the two mutually exclusive).
    - **Cloud backup data-loss (2 blockers).** The Account-mode export on both apps omitted the `goal_progress` table, so a Replace-import computed an empty keep-set and DELETED every quantitative daily number. Fix: both cloud exports now emit `habitProgress` (paged read, degrading to empty pre-migration so export never fails whole).
    - **Pre-migration edit break (1 high).** Mobile Account `updateHabit` force-wrote `verify_effective_from`/`verify_conditions = null` ungated for every manual habit → PGRST204 on a pre-migration project, failing the common rename/recolour edit. Fix: gated behind `compoundVerificationEnabled` (flips only after those migrations apply), mirroring the existing `target` gate.
  - *Tech Notes*: `mobile/lib/providers/goal_provider.dart` (sweep guard + `setProgress` verdict guard + verify-null gate), `mobile/lib/ui/screens/privacy_settings_screen.dart` + `desktop/lib/features/settings/presentation/settings_page.dart` (goal_progress export), `desktop/lib/features/dashboard/application/dashboard_controller.dart` (sweep + `setHabitProgressForDay` guards). Added a QA diagnostic: `mobile/lib/core/private_local_database.dart` logs the stored `user_version` vs `PrivateDbSchema.version` at open (visible in the in-app log viewer) so the real on-device schema version and the v6→v11 migration are confirmable. +2 blocker-regression tests (verified habit never swept / never verdict-clobbered by manual progress). Suites green: mobile 579, desktop 588, evolve_sync 235, evolve_targets 107. No new deps; no native/Xcode work.

- [2026-07-24]: Habit classes — desktop tracking-mode picker (Checkbox / Number, locked Automatic)
  - *Details*: Mirrored mobile's mutually-exclusive tracking-mode picker in the desktop habit editor, with the deliberate macOS asymmetry: a Mac can author only Checkbox / Number and can DISPLAY a synced iPhone-verified habit read-only but never author or change its rule. Editing a habit whose `verificationRule != null` now shows a LOCKED read-only "Automatic" row (label `trackingMode.automaticLocked` = "Verified — edit on iPhone") with no target field and no Checkbox/Number choice, so a desktop save can never wipe an iPhone-authored HealthKit/Screen Time rule. Non-verified habits get a 2-segment Checkbox / Number picker (Number only surfaces while `DesktopTargetsConfig.enabled` — currently dark, so a plain habit stays a plain checkbox with no picker, preserving the one-tap add flow).
  - *Tech Notes*: `desktop/lib/features/habits/presentation/habits_page.dart` — new top-level `enum HabitTrackingMode { checkbox, number, automatic }`; `_HabitEditorDialog` gains `late HabitTrackingMode _trackingMode` derived on init (`verificationRule != null → automatic; else target != null → number; else checkbox`). The old `if (DesktopTargetsConfig.enabled)` `TargetField` block is replaced by the tracking-mode section: locked-Automatic branch renders new private `_LockedAutomaticRow` (shieldCheck + `trackingMode.automaticLocked` + lock glyph); non-verified branch reuses the kit's `EvolveSegmentedControl<HabitTrackingMode>` (Checkbox clears `_target`, Number seeds `TargetPresetCatalog.countDaily.targetWith()` and renders the `TargetField`). `desktop/lib/features/habits/presentation/target_field.dart` — added `bool showNone = true`; the editor passes `showNone: false` in Number mode so the segmented control is the single source of truth for checkbox-vs-number (the standalone `TargetField` test still taps "Simple"). New i18n `trackingMode` object (title/checkbox/number/automatic/automaticLocked) in all five locales (en/it/es/de/ar; ar machine MSA, flagged for review); slang regenerated. No feature-flag values touched, no new deps, no schema/migration, no native/Xcode work. **Verification rule survives a desktop edit** (traced + relied on): `controller.updateHabit` calls `habit.copyWith(target:…, clearTarget: target==null)` without `clearVerificationRule` (defaults false) so `verificationRule`/`verifyEffectiveFrom`/`additionalConditions`/`verificationJoin` all pass through untouched; `stampTargetEffectiveFrom` only touches target fields; `private_dashboard_repository.updateHabit` re-writes the verification columns from the preserved habit. `flutter analyze` clean on both touched files (7 pre-existing issues elsewhere unchanged); full desktop suite green (588 tests, baseline unchanged). On-device QA pending (no Xcode/simulator on this Mac).

- [2026-07-24]: Habit classes — mobile tracking-mode picker (Checkbox / Number / Automatic)
  - *Details*: Replaced the additive optional-fields layout in the habit create/edit modal with a mutually-exclusive tracking-mode picker — a habit is exactly one class: plain Checkbox, a manual Number (quantitative target), or an Automatic (auto-verified) rule. Choosing a class clears the others' state, so a habit can never carry both a target AND a rule (the both-flags-on incoherence the pre-flip audit flagged). Default is Checkbox; the one-tap fast-add is unchanged. The picker shows only the enabled classes (Checkbox always; Number iff `TargetsConfig.enabled`; Automatic iff `VerificationConfig.enabled`) and hides entirely when only Checkbox is available. Because verification is already live, this DOES change the single-metric verification creation UX — the inline "Auto-verify" switch becomes the "Automatic" segment (`VerificationRuleField(showSwitch:false)`, seeded with a default template so it renders). A synced habit whose true class is disabled by a local flag (a mixed-version fleet) shows a LOCKED read-only row instead of a mislabelled fallback picker, so tapping a segment can't silently wipe the synced target/rule — the mobile mirror of desktop's locked Automatic row (closes the one medium finding from the adversarial Phase 2 check).
  - *Tech Notes*: `mobile/lib/ui/widgets/habit_management_modal.dart` — new top-level `enum HabitTrackingMode {checkbox,number,automatic}` + `_trackingModeLabel` + `_LockedClassRow`; `_trackingMode` state derived at every (re)init (`_modeFor`: rule→automatic, target→number, else checkbox); `_enabledModes` getter; `_onTrackingModeChanged` clears the other class and seeds a default (countDaily target / first-template rule); `classLocked` renders `_LockedClassRow` when the class isn't locally enabled; by-mode field render replaces the two additive `if(...Config.enabled)` blocks. `target_field.dart` gained `showNone` (false in Number mode, so the segmented control is the sole checkbox-vs-number owner). i18n `targets.trackingMode.{title,checkbox,number,automatic,locked}` in all 5 locales (ar machine MSA, flagged); slang regenerated (mobile `translations*.g.dart` git-ignored). No feature-flag values changed, no schema/migration, no native/Xcode work. Verified independently: mobile 579 green, `flutter analyze` clean on touched files. Adversarial Phase 2 check: 1 medium finding, fixed. On-device QA pending.

No other code change.

- [2026-07-24]: Habit classes — Phase 4: widen CI to the shared packages + migrations
  - *Details*: The shared pure-Dart packages (`evolve_sync`, `evolve_targets`, `evolve_verification`) and the SQL `migrations/` previously ran ZERO CI — the only Flutter CI was scoped to `mobile/**`, so a schema-migration or reconcile bug (exactly the class this work touched) could land unguarded. Added a `packages` matrix job (pub get + `analyze --fatal-infos` + `flutter test` per package) and widened the trigger paths to include `packages/**` and `migrations/**`, so a migration change also re-runs the mobile `schema_drift_test.dart` guard. The Arabic (ar) `trackingMode` strings on both apps are flagged for native review in TO_SIMO_DO.md (machine MSA), same batch as the earlier `targets.*` / `verification.compound.*` strings.
  - *Tech Notes*: `.github/workflows/mobile-ci.yml` — YAML validated; jobs `analyze-and-test` (unchanged) + new `packages` (matrix over the 3 packages). No app code change. (Desktop still has no CI — a reasonable follow-up, out of this cycle's scope.)
